# W1 specification: the space algebra (ontology chart, wave W1; Draft stage, 2026-11-23; amended, pass 1, pass 2, pass 2b and pass 3, 2026-11-23)

Status: DRAFT, amended by pass 3 (`scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check3/check_design_layer.md`, the independent check of the design layer, and the orchestrator's rulings `P3-1` to `P3-7` on its findings, recorded in the last section) and by pass 2b (`scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amendment_decisions_2b.md`: the orchestrator's rulings `P2b-1` to `P2b-10` on the open decisions SD-28 to SD-42 of pass 2, recorded in the last section) and by pass 2 (`scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amendment_decisions_2.md`: rulings `P2-1` to `P2-15`, recorded section by section in the last section; it finishes the DESIGN LAYER, that is sections 1 to 5, 6.0, 6.0a and 8 to 12, and leaves each unit's sections 6.x and 7.x marked NOT FINAL, to be finalised at the unit's opening from `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_<unit>.md`) and by pass 1 (`scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amendment_decisions_1.md`: decisions A1 to A8, B1 to B4, C1 to C5 and D, recorded section by section in the last section, "Amendment record"), for the user's checkpoint; nothing here is ruled by the user beyond what section 10 and the decisions record say. Written on `refactor/ontology` at `b5f18d4e`. The binding inputs are the synthesis `scratch/ontology/w1/synth/synthesis.md` (D1 to D32, cited "D#"), amended by `synth/attack2/attack2.md` (cited "A2 <finding id>", for example A2 A3 is attack 2's finding A3; the chart's own rulings are cited "chart ruling A2", "chart ruling B7"), the probes `synth/p1/p1_variance.md` and `synth/p2/p2_energy_metric.md`, the rulings of `ontology_chart.md` (O1 to O40, A1 to A3, B7 to B12), `true_ends.md` (R1 to R17) and `posing_u4_direct_sum_space.md`. Where a design panel file disagrees with a decision, the decision wins. Measurements taken for this specification are under `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/` (`measure_inverse_bytes.md`, `measure_trace_metric.md`, `measure_callsites.md`, `measure_pairings.md`, `measure_coarse_balance.md`, and `final/incidence_final.py` with its `.json`; the amendment pass adds `amend1/`: the incidence re-run on the new unit boundaries `incidence_amend1.py`, the legacy-class readings `legacy_inherit2.py`, `legacy_arms.py`, the spies `plug/legacy_spy.py`, `plug/axisw_spy.py`, `plug/sign_spy.py`, `plug/hp6_spy.py`, the census `zero_weight_census.py` and the checker `check_spec.py`). The checks that motivated the pass are `check_elegance.md` (findings cited "E#") and `check_structure.md` (cited "S#" with the words "of the structure check", to keep them apart from this specification's section objects `S1` to `S13`).

Marker use is `articulation` section 5: `[M]` measured, with its instrument named; `[R]` reasoned and not measured; `[HYPOTHESIS]`. A bare number is read as measured. A gate's "first red" is `[M]` only where an input that exists in today's tree was run and read red; where the only red today is that a module does not exist (an `ImportError`), the text says "import-only red" and the behavioural red is the first one the gate can show after its unit lands, with the mutation that produces it.

Words defined once. Identifier families (amendment B4): the done-when rows are `W1-P1` to `W1-P10`; the hand pairings of `measure_pairings.md` are `HP1` to `HP10`; this section 2.1's objects are `S1` to `S13`; this specification's own decisions are `SD-1` and on (section 11); the synthesis's decisions are `D1` to `D32`; the chart's rulings keep their `O`, `A`, `B`, `R`, `U` ids; the probe of the variance is cited by its file, `p1_variance`. A **factor** is one tensor slot: an axis (index set with an optional form) together with its variance and its subquotient (section 2). An **axis** is the generator's object: label, index set, form, origin. A **form** on an axis is its reference measure: `NoForm`, `Diag(w)` (an existing `DiagonalMetric`) or `Dense(G)` (an existing `DenseMetric`). A **subquotient** `S/K` of an axis's coordinate space is a quotient of one declared subspace `S` by another `K` inside it (S5). A **restricted axis** is a new axis on a subset of another axis's index set (3.2). A **kernel** of a form is the null space of its symmetric matrix. A **declaration** is an exact description of a subset of an axis's coordinate space (section 2.3). The **Riesz map** (`riesz`) identifies a function with its integral against the form; the **dual** (`dual`) is the functional dual. A **chart** is how a measure or function is stored: as a density (values against the form) or integrated (masses). **Slot order** is the order of a space's factors. **Layer** and **phase** are those of the concept table (`docs/architecture/concept_table.toml`); every class added here lives in `orpheus/numerics/` (layer `numerics`, phase `structure`) unless the module path says `transport/`. The rulings of amendment pass 2 are cited `P2-1` to `P2-15` and those of pass 2b `P2b-1` to `P2b-10`; the findings of the per-unit check of the amended text are cited "check 2" with the check's name (`elegance` or `structure`), the unit and the finding's id, for example "check 2 structure N03 F4" (files `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/check_<elegance|structure>_<scope>.md`), and the open findings of a unit are in its opening file `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_<unit>.md`. A **declared edge** is a tagged `SCOPE-BOUNDARY` or `ELEGANCE-DEBT` comment naming the machinery or the wave that retires it (`coding-standards`). **Index shape** is the shape of an axis's own index set; an axis of index shape `()` (rank 0) is a one-point factor that adds no array axis (3.2).

## 1. Outcome and done-when

**Outcome.** The space algebra of the ontology chart, wave W1. Every tensor slot is one kind of object, a `Factor`, carrying its variance (kind and chart) and a subquotient of its coordinate space, and reading its form from one home, the axis. There is one tensor product (`TensorSpace`) and one direct sum (`DirectSumSpace`) as the only two arms of `FunctionSpace`. The pairing is one object, `DualPairing`. The boundary trace is a direct sum over faces minted by one builder. The units are built leaves first (section 6), so no type is built twice and no production factor ever carries a placeholder variance. The four transport spaces are derived from a trial and test pair, `TrialTest`, and are not declared. The dense inverse and the half-range have one owner each and no hidden cutoff. Operators on true ends (W2), members (W3), transport atoms (W4), Problem and Solution (W5), geometry and faces (W6), the boundary algebra (W7) and measures and generators (W8a) are not built here; section 9 states what each reads from W1 and may not spell.

**The wave table's done-when row, re-worded from the measurements** (the row of `ontology_chart.md` line 449 is a `[HYPOTHESIS]`; this text replaces it). Each predicate has one command, run from the repository root with `.venv/bin/python -O -m pytest -p no:cacheprovider --color=no <path>`; the gate files are specified in section 7.

## 2. The algebra in symbols

Notation. Real finite-dimensional spaces. `I` is an ordered finite index set; `R^I` its coordinate space with the Euclidean pairing `<.,.>`. `V*` is the dual. For a symmetric positive semidefinite `G` on `R^I` write `ker G` for its null space. A *declaration* `d` of `R^I` is a subset description (2.3); `Ann d` is its annihilator in `(R^I)*`, identified with `R^I` by the Euclidean pairing, so `Ann Coord(J) = Coord(I minus J)` and `Ann Ann d = d`.

### 2.1 Objects

**S1 Index set and layout.** `I` finite and ordered. Product `I x J` (row-major) and coproduct `I (+) J` (offsets `0, |I|`). A `Layout` is an index set with its flat order and product and coproduct (concept `layout`).

**S2 Form.** A form on `I` is one of: `NoForm` (no reference measure: no density, no flat, no sharp, no norm); `Diag(w)` with `w` in `R^I`; `Dense(G)` with `G` symmetric. `Diag(1)` (counting) is a form and is not `NoForm`. Each present form has a signature `(n_+, n_0, n_-)` and a kernel `ker G`. `Diag` reads both exactly from the signs of `w`; for a `Dense` form the declared `kernel` implies them, `(n - kernel.dim, kernel.dim, 0)`: the constructor takes the kernel and no signature, so no negative direction is representable by the declaration, and the check that the Gram has none (a floating-point test, so with a tolerance) is the gate G01d (amendment A6; D7 names the signature as a constructor argument, and the kernel declaration carries the same information). A form with `n_- > 0` is refused (the Krein arm is named, not built, D7). The declared kernel is a compare field of `DenseMetric`, so it is part of the form's content identity (P2-6): two Grams with equal matrices and different declared kernels are different forms, and every space that holds a dense form changes its identity digest at N01 (6.0a).

**S3 Axis.** `a = (label, I, form, origin, restriction)` plus derived `kind` (nodal or modal), `support`, `nodes`, `layout`. Identity: content digest of `(type, label, shape, form content, kind, support, nodes, restriction)`; `origin` is provenance and not identity (as today, `numerics/axis.py:461`); `restriction` is `None` except on a restricted axis, where it records the parent axis's identity key and the index subset, so that a restricted axis is a NEW axis on the subset with its parent in its identity (amendment A2, 3.2). The axis carries no variance. An axis may have index shape `()` (rank 0, one point): it contributes no array axis (P2-2; 3.2).

**S4 Variance.** `Variance = (kind, chart)` with `kind` in `{FUNCTION, MEASURE}` and `chart` in `{DENSITY, INTEGRATED}`. Kind is intrinsic to the index set (a function pulls back along an index map, a measure pushes forward; R16). The chart is the Radon-Nikodym identification of functions with measures, `f -> f w`, which exists only on an axis whose form is present. Two involutions on the four values: `dual(k, c) = (flip k, flip c)`, `riesz(k, c) = (k, flip c)`. They generate the Klein four-group `{id, dual, riesz, dual.riesz}` on `Variance` alone (4 values, no constraint).

**S5 Subquotient.** `sk = S/K` with declarations `K` inside `S` inside `Coord(I)`. The default is the whole space, `S = Coord(I)`, `K = Coord(empty)`. `dual` and `riesz` both send `S/K` to `(Ann K)/(Ann S)` (the coordinates are written in the stored chart; the Euclidean identification of `R^I` with its dual is the one fixed by S2's convention, A2 1b).

**Stored coordinates (amendment A2).** The stored coordinates of a factor are ALWAYS on the axis's full index set, whatever `S/K` is: a member of `S/K` is stored as a representative that vanishes off `S` and is free on `K`; a member of `(Ann K)/(Ann S)` vanishes on `K` and is free on `Ann S`. So `X.dual()` and `X.riesz(...)` keep `X`'s stored shape, and no stored array changes when the trace is typed (D21). The dimension of `S/K` is `dim S - dim K`. A view that stores only the rows of `S` is not a subquotient: it is a restricted axis (S3, 3.2).

**S6 Factor.** `f = (axis, variance, sk)`. The stored shape of `f` is the axis's shape for every `S/K` (the convention of S5, amendment A2); its dimension is `dim S - dim K`. For the whole space both equal `|I|`, unchanged from today (D11, D21).
- **Invariant A0** (energy law): if `axis.form` is `NoForm` then `chart = DENSITY` iff `kind = FUNCTION`. On a formless axis kind and chart are one bit; `riesz` is therefore undefined there, and the Klein group there is `{id, dual}`.
- **Constructibility replaces a legality test (P2-1, which supersedes amendment A4's legality test at `riesz`).** Invariant of `Factor` (S6c): where the axis has a form, the induced form of S7 on `S/K` is nondegenerate, that is `S ∩ ker G ⊆ K` on the DENSITY end, and an INTEGRATED factor is constructible iff its density partner (its `riesz` image) is. A factor that breaks it is refused at construction, with the message naming the kernel to quotient by. In particular a whole `sk` on an axis whose form is singular cannot be built, and the builder states `V/kernel`: no factor is built with a default `sk` on a singular axis. Then the Klein four-group acts on every constructible formed factor, L3's `dual∘dual = id` is unconditional, and there is no separate legality test at `riesz`. `[M]` (A2 A3, the exhaustive enumeration, on the 4-coordinate axis with a 1-dimensional kernel): 324 states, 81 pairs `(S, K)` with `K ⊆ S` times the 4 variances; 216 construct and carry the group law (54 pairs: the kernel index is not in `S`, or is in `S` and in `K`), 108 are refused at construction (27 pairs: the kernel index is in `S` and not in `K`). `[R]` Consequence for production: a formed factor whose form has a zero weight and a whole `sk` can no longer be built, so dead-mode frame axes and zero-weight test fixtures are affected, not only quadratures; the census of such mints is owed at the openings of N03 and N04 (section 12, items 16 and 17).

**S7 Induced form.** The form of a DENSITY factor on `S/K` is the coset minimum: `q(x + K) = min_{k in K} (x+k)^T G (x+k)` on `S`, a Schur complement of `G` restricted to `S` by `K`. It is defined for every `S`, `K` (G semidefinite) and is nondegenerate exactly when `S ∩ ker G ⊆ K` `[M]` A2 B2: two sections of `V/span(1)` for a dense `G` differ by 11.0 while the coset minimum differs by 1.3e-15; the dual of `Sub(S)` with `G_SS` has the form `(G_SS)^-1`, the Schur complement of `G^-1`, matching to 2.2e-16 while the coordinate restriction `(G^-1)_SS` is off by 0.957. **The chart of the form (amendment A4):** on a DENSITY factor the form is the coset minimum `q` above; on an INTEGRATED factor it is the inverse of the induced form of its `riesz` partner (which is a density factor): for the dual of `Sub(S)` that is `(G_SS)^-1`, not the coordinate restriction. The form is therefore induced for every factor `Factor.__post_init__` accepts, on a `Coord`, a `Spanned` or an `Ann` declaration alike; a subquotient whose form cannot be induced is refused at construction. The induced form is stored on the axis's full index set (S5) and its kernel is `K ∪ (I minus S)` for a `Coord` pair and the generator's declared `Spanned` kernel for `V/Spanned`; on the integrated end it is the inverse face built by the existing constructors (`DenseMetric.inverse_of`, `DualMetric`, the masked reciprocal), never a recomputed `1/w` (P2-4, 3.1).

**S8 Form blocks.** A *form home* of a space is a partition of its slot labels into form blocks. Each block has one form and, on `riesz`, one chart. W1 builds only singleton blocks, so chart is per slot on everything W1 builds. A block with more than one slot is named and is W3's (the curvilinear in-cell Gram is fibred over cells, not a Kronecker product: `[M]` A2 C2, Kronecker `sigma_2/sigma_1` = 3.3e-2, Cartesian 1.1e-16).

**S9 Tensor space.** `S = <f_1, ..., f_n>`, `n >= 0`, labels unique; as a vector space `(x)_i (R^{|S_i|})` with the factor's chart. The empty list is the unit `1 = R`.

**S10 Direct sum.** `D = (+)_l (label_l, S_l)` with one or more labelled summands (`[M]` a solid radial mesh has one traced face, `mesh/axis.py:393`, so the lower bound is 1). Summands may be direct sums. Labels are part of the identity.

**S11 The pair.** `TrialTest = (trial, test, testing)`: `trial` and `test` are spaces (a tensor space or a direct sum of them, recursing on the sum tree), `testing` maps each `(summand path, label)` to `NODE` or `INTEGRAL` (a one-level space has the empty path; 3.8, SD-42); `NODE` requires a present form on that label's factor. On every label the kind of `trial` is opposite to the kind of `test`. `test` is a declared space (the test chart is not a function of the trial: the ordinate layout keeps the angular chart, the moment layout flips it, D15; the moment layout's test is `test_space(frame).dual()`, derived from the frame's one integrated factor, 3.4a and SD-35(a)).

**S13 The completion map `e_V` (P2-12).** For a space `V`, `V.euclidean()` is a different space (3.4) whose `NoForm` forms are replaced by counting, and `e_V : V → V.euclidean()` is the identity on stored coordinates. It commutes with every coordinate verb (`flat_on` and `sharp_on` on the formed labels, `dual(on=)` and `riesz(on=)` on the formed labels, `pack`, `unpack`, `partition_by`) and it is not a Riesz map: it changes the form and keeps the kind and the chart. `Field.l2`, `Field.inner_product` and the other member norms that reach `space.norm` today are defined through it, `norm_V(x) = V.euclidean().norm(e_V(x))`; no verb `euclidean_norm` is added. Without `e_V` the membership check of `coordinates_of` (`numerics/space.py:735-739`) refuses a member of `V` handed to `V.euclidean().norm`, which is why the map is an object of the algebra. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n11_normspy.py` (a method-wrapping spy over `tests/gates/sn/test_solution_read.py`, 15 passed, `check_structure_N11.md` premise): `numerics/iteration.py:808` alone makes 4,171 `norm` calls on spaces with an energy axis in that one file. `[R]` the map and its commutation are not yet objects in the tree; the consumer rows of `scratch/ontology/w1/synth/p2/p2_energy_metric.md` (N rows: `field.py:377`, `iteration.py:808`, `angular_residual.py:172,:178`, `diffusion/solver.py:645,:646`; P rows: `reaction_rate_functional.py:186,:231`, `diffusion/solver.py:264`, `homogeneous/solver.py:578`, `field.py:387`) go into the opening file of N11. A gate for the law is owed (L17, opening of N11).

**S12 The pairing.** `DualPairing(left, right)` is an element of `(left (x) right)*`: the full contraction. Per factor the kinds must be opposite; per form block, charts opposite give the canonical contraction, both `DENSITY` give the form leg `G`, both `INTEGRATED` are refused (R15). Per factor the subquotients must match: the canonical leg requires `right.sk == left.sk.dual()`, the form leg requires `right.sk == left.sk`, and anything else is refused ("pairing of mismatched subquotients": a pairing whose value moves with the representative is unspellable).

### 2.2 The laws, each with its converse

Each row is a law, its converse (or the statement that none holds and why), and the state it makes unspellable. "Constructible" is S6's predicate; there is no separate legality test at `riesz` (P2-1).

| # | law | converse or sharpness | unspellable state |
|---|---|---|---|
| L1 | `(x)` on slot lists is concatenation; `(S (x) T) (x) U = S (x) (T (x) U)`; `1 (x) S = S`. `of_axes(a, b)` and `of_axes(a) * of_axes(b)` are one value of one type | the decomposition is not unique as a list of factors only up to the same flat list: `a (x) b = c (x) d` iff the slot lists are equal; `T (x) S` is a permutation of `S (x) T`, an operator (W2), not an identity | two types for one product |
| L2 | `(S (x) T)* = S* (x) T*`, slot-wise, order kept | does not hold as `T* (x) S*` | a dual of a tensor with permuted slots |
| L3 | On a constructible factor (S6): `dual^2 = id` and `riesz^2 = id` unconditionally, `dual.riesz = riesz.dual`, and the constructible set is closed under `dual` and `riesz` (constructibility is one predicate on the density end, S6, and closure is its consequence); the four maps form the Klein group on the constructible set. `[M]` A2 A3, exhaustive on a 4-coordinate axis with a 1-dimensional kernel: 216 of 324 states (the `(S, K)` pairs times the 4 variances) construct, and on the 216 `riesz.riesz = id` (216 of 216), the set is closed under `dual` (216 of 216), and `dual` and `riesz` commute (216 of 216); the other 108 are refused at construction (P2-1) | the converse fails off the constructible set (the 108 states are refused), and the narrower rule `K = S ∩ ker G` leaves only 8 of 64 with `riesz.riesz = id`. On the full face slot `Diag(abs(cos) w)` (`S = V`, `K = empty`, `ker G` the tangential set) the factor is refused at construction, with the message naming the tangential kernel (P2-1): flat then sharp would miss the input by 1.996, on `V/K` by 2.2e-16 `[M]` A2 A1, measured on the coordinates of `S` minus `K` (member equality is modulo `K`, 2.4). On a nonsingular `Diag(w)` the round trip is exact in R and differs in the last bit on 175 of 1600 entries in floating point `[M]` A2: a chart change is not free in bytes (N00 pins this) | a Riesz leg on a singular slot that silently zeroes the kernel (the Moore-Penrose section, 1.996 off); a formed factor on a singular form with no declared quotient |
| L4 | `dual(on=L)` flips kind, chart and `sk` on the slots of `L`, and leaves the rest; `riesz(on=L)` flips chart and `sk`. `d_L d_M = d_{L triangle M}`; `d_all = d()`. A label not in the space is refused | `d_L S = S` iff `L` is empty | a partial flip of an unknown label |
| L5 | (the refusals of whole-space `flat`, `sharp`, `norm`, `inner_product` are in force from N11, 3.4) `riesz(on=L)` is typed iff `L` is a union of form blocks and none is `NoForm` (a constructible formed factor is `riesz`-legal, S6). Whole-space `flat` and `sharp` are the coordinate maps `flat_on(all labels)` and `sharp_on(all labels)` of `riesz(on=all labels)` and are refused iff any block is `NoForm` | `norm` and `inner_product` have no partial form (a partial flat gives no scalar): they are refused whole when any block is `NoForm`, with a message naming `euclidean()` | a Riesz map on energy; a norm of a formless space |
| L6 | Direct sum: `(+)_l S_l`'s `dual(on=L)` and `riesz(on=L)` act summand-wise. A flip addressed to a label is refused whole if any summand holding the label refuses, and refused if no summand holds it. Summand labels and factor labels are two namespaces: a flip addresses factor labels; a summand is addressed by `summand(label)` | `[M]` A2 A5, before P2-1: with one summand legal (`V/tang`) and one a full singular slot the block-diagonal form is degenerate, so the whole flip must refuse; after P2-1 that second summand cannot be constructed, and the refusal the law still needs is a summand whose addressed slot is `NoForm` (a flip of `energy` addressed to a sum whose summands hold a `NoForm` energy slot): a half-applied flip stays unspellable (gate owed at N05) | a half-applied flip |
| L7 | Slot-wise constructibility of a tensor of subquotients agrees with nondegeneracy of the product form `G_1 (x) G_2` on the quotient on 1971 of 2187 pairs of slot subquotients `[M]` A2 A5; the 216 exceptions all have a zero-dimensional factor | the product form accepts the zero-dimensional exceptions and the slot rule refuses them; a tensor of subquotients has kernel `K_1 (x) S_2 + S_1 (x) K_2`, not a rectangle `[M]` A2 B4, so no space-level `(S, K)` exists | a space-level subquotient |

It does not cover a block that is not a Kronecker product | a leg order that depends on construction history |
| L9 | Pairing legs per block: kinds opposite (the kind check runs first, per factor); charts opposite is canonical, both `DENSITY` is the form leg `G_b`, both `INTEGRATED` is refused. Per factor the subquotients must match (canonical leg: `right.sk == left.sk.dual()`; form leg: `right.sk == left.sk`; otherwise refused). On a `NoForm` block, opposite kinds force opposite charts (A0), so the leg is canonical | `<x, y> = <y, x>` under swapping the operands (the legs are symmetric); no converse beyond that. `[M]` `check_structure_N09.md` F2 (reading `synth/p1/p1_variance.md:16`): `(flux, source)` and `(source, source)` are refused by KIND on the angular slot first (both `MEASURE` on all three slots); the R15 refusal on energy is the K-none reading of the probe, and the K-slot reading, which W1 builds, adds the kind refusal. R15 is reached only on opposite kinds that are both integrated: `(response, source)` on the spatial slot (7.13), where angular takes the form leg and energy the canonical leg | the pairing of two integrated factors; a function with a function |
| L10 | `flat(x) = pairing(x, .)`, the partial application (D17): a `Covector`, the same object as `DualPairing.covector(x)` on the form legs; `flat_on(L)` is its coordinate map on the labels `L` (it keeps the kind, flips the chart and applies the form of each block of `L`). `sharp` is its inverse on the constructible set. The musical map `V -> V.dual()` (canonical on a formless slot, `flat_on(formed)` on a formed one) and the chart change `V -> V.riesz()` share a matrix on the formed slots and are two morphisms with different codomains (check 2 structure N11 F7). There is no second spelling of the contraction: the one body is `flat_on(labels)` with `sharp_on(labels)` its inverse, and `flat`, `sharp`, `apply_metric`, `apply_inverse_metric`, `integral` and `DualPairing.covector` delegate to them by a stated call that gates G04l and G09f assert by `ast` | `sharp . flat = id` on `S/K`, that is equal on the coordinates of `S` minus `K` for a `Coord` `K`, on constructible slots (L3) | a second spelling of the full contraction (`InnerProductFunctional`'s one-axis retraction is not a full contraction: it stays under `ELEGANCE-DEBT[retraction] W2`, P2b-8) |
| L11 | The pair derives the four spaces: `flux = trial`, `importance = test`, `source = test.dual().riesz(on = labels with testing NODE)`, `response = trial.dual().riesz(on = labels with testing NODE)`. There is no stored forward kind: it is the trial's kinds. Round trip: `source.riesz(on=NODE labels).dual() = test` and `response.riesz(on=NODE labels).dual() = trial` | `[M]` probe `p1_variance`: 12 of 12 cells of R14 reproduced, plus the three witnesses of A1; the four spaces are uniform in kind (12 of 12 cells). Table in 7.13 (NOT FINAL: as of its draft, re-derived at N08's opening), gate `test_four_spaces.py` | a source space declared by hand |
| L12 | Half-range: for a face with outward axis cosines `c_n` (a generator datum: `sign * quadrature.mean_axis_cosine(axis_index)`), `outflow = {n : c_n > 0}`, `inflow = {n : c_n < 0}`, `tangential = {n : c_n = 0}`, exact comparisons with no tolerance and non-finite cosines refused; the three sets partition the ordinates | a generator of a rotated rule must emit exact zeros: `[M]` every shipped tangential cosine is exactly 0.0 (24 of 24 axes, `c1`), and the owner "fixed points of the reflection" refuses 3 of 29 rule-and-axis pairs that today's trace builds (`q1`, `q1b`) so it is not used | six cutoffs for one half-range (`TANGENTIAL_EPS`, `_OUTFLOW_COSINE_TOL`, `_TANGENTIAL_MU`, `_MU_DIRECTION_EPS` (P2-7), and `sn/sweep/cache.py:355`'s literal `1e-15` with `_OCTANT_SIGN_EPS` (P2b-5), `[R]` six until N02's opening read confirms that each decides the sign or the zero of a direction cosine; the census is `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2/p2_7_cutoff_census.out`) |

The views carry the factor's own variance | the converse: where `K` meets a half (a rule with a zero weight on a non-tangential ordinate) `R-` reads a gauge coordinate. `[M]` `check2/n06_probe3.py`: on such a synthetic rule `K = {1, 2}`, `inflow = {0, 1}`, `K ∩ inflow = {1}` and `dim V/K = 3` against `n_out + n_in = 4`; the trace builder refuses it with the fragment "K meets a half-range" `[R]` | a half-range view that reads a gauge coordinate |
| L17 | Completion (P2-12, S13): `e_V` commutes with every coordinate verb, and `V.euclidean().norm(e_V x)^2` is the sum over the formed blocks of the form's quadratic form plus the counting sum over the `NoForm` blocks | `e_V` is not `riesz`: it changes the form and keeps kind and chart; `V.euclidean().norm` applied to a member of `V` without `e_V` is refused by the membership check | a norm of a formless space; a completed norm applied to an uncompleted member |

Both pairings `<importance, source>` and `<response, flux>` are preserved. The rule of each slot is read from the variances of a `FourSpaces` built by `orpheus` | `[M]` toy (`scratch/ontology/w1/spec/coarse_balance_toy.py`, run for pass 2 by `check_structure_N08.md` F8 and `n08_l14_literal.py`): the function-summed mutation moves both pairings far above `1e-2` on the non-uniform merge; the MEAN-chart mutation moves both pairings (0.360 and 0.340 non-uniform, 0.500 uniform) and the balance equation (residual 1.56 non-uniform against 5.6e-16 uniform); the swapped-roles mutation leaves both pairings exact and the balance equation catches it (0.431 non-uniform; the toy draws `q` and the cross sections at each call, so a quoted residual is one draw and the gate asserts thresholds, `> 1e-2` and `< 1e-13`, `[R]` until the toy seeds its draws); summing the DENSITY slot of the spatial factor, which L14 read as "a measure sums" before pass 2, leaves both pairings exact (1.9e-16 and 1.2e-16) and moves the coarse flux mass by 0.88 non-uniform and 1.00 uniform, so the gate gains a mass arm: the pairing of the coarse flux with the unit function (the form leg `V_I`) equals the fine mass. Pairings alone cannot discriminate: the gate is the pairings, the balance equation and the mass arm | a merge that sums a function or a density |
| L15 | Face area and orientation (R9): the face-spatial factor of a trace summand has form `Diag(A_f)` (a rank-0 factor, index shape `()`, on a 1-D mesh, P2-2). The angular factor's form is `Diag(abs(cos) * w)`. The product is `abs(Omega.n) w A_f`. The leakage of a face is the pairing of the face flux with `sigma_f ⊗ 1_E ⊗ 1_X`: the form leg on the angular slot, the canonical leg on energy, the form leg `Diag(A_f)` on the face-spatial slot (after N10), where `sigma_f = sgn(c_n)` is the sign of the outward cosine, an element of the function space on the angular index set (`Orientation.sign()`, 3.6; amendment A3). `A_f` enters once, and the sign enters through the partner function and never through the metric: a retraction by `Diag(abs(cos) * w)` alone is orientation-blind and computes `J+ + J-`, not the net current `J+ - J-`. The net current of a face is the same pairing over the angular slot alone: `AngularBoundaryFlux.net_current(face)` becomes the pairing with the orientation's sign over the angular slot (codomain `current(summand)`) and the leakage is built on it, with no second spelling (P2-11). `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend1/sign_report.out` (a spy on `AngularBoundaryFlux.net_current` over the sphere-albedo rows at `alpha = 0.3` and `0.7`, 24 calls, the inflow rows nonzero on 24 of 24): the orientation-blind sum is `(1 + alpha)/(1 - alpha)` times today's `net_current` (1.857 and 5.667), and the signed sum equals it to 2.99e-16 (an identity: it is the code's own expression) | The trace measure is the boundary measure that the volume measure induces (`SNSolver._face_area_of`'s own docstring: "so the balance identity closes"); this converse is gated by the balance residual of a converged fixed-source solve on the sphere, the cylinder and the 3-D box, red under `counting(1)` and under `Diag(A_f**2)` (gate owed, opening of N10). `riesz(on={"spatial"})` is defined only if `A_f > 0`, and a face at `r = 0` is refused; no shipped fixture traces one (`measure_trace_metric.md` (a): the sphere traces `xmax` only). `[M]` `measure_trace_metric.md`: today `|mu| w` omits `A_f` on 5 of 5 SN fixtures; `A_f != 1` on the sphere, cylinder and every non-unit box, `= 1` on every slab | the area applied outside the metric; the orientation in the metric; a second spelling of the net current |

### 2.3 The closed sums and their normal forms

- `FunctionSpace` has exactly two arms: `TensorSpace` and `DirectSumSpace`. No subclass of an arm (D14). `FunctionSpace` remains the common abstract type (53 production files and 107 test files annotate an end with it, `measure_callsites.md` section 1).
- `Form = NoForm | DiagonalMetric | DenseMetric` (the one definition of `Form`, a named closed sum in `numerics/metric.py`; every consumer matches on it exhaustively and no second definition exists). `FactoredMetric` and `DualMetric` stay as the derived product form and the form of a dual slot. `NoForm` is a frozen field-less dataclass, because it becomes `Axis` identity content and `values/content.py` encodes no plain object (`[M]` `check_structure_N03.md` F6: `_encode` raises `ContentlessError`).
- `Declaration = Coord | Spanned | Ann` (new, `numerics/declaration.py`; `ContentIdentity` classes rostered at N01, P2-6). `Coord(n, indices)` is an exact index subset of `R^n` (sorted, unique, in range). `Spanned(n, key, dim)` is a subspace the generator knows by a content `key`, of dimension `dim`, and its numeric basis is never stored in the declaration. `Ann(n, of)` is the annihilator. Normal form: `Ann(Ann(d)) = d`; `Ann(Coord(n, J)) = Coord(n, complement)`. Inclusion `d ⊆ e` is decidable exactly when both are `Coord`, or `d` is `Coord(n, empty)`, or `e` is `Coord(n, all)`, or `d = e`; otherwise `contains` raises (SD-25) and every rule that needs it refuses (the generator must declare the result). Normal form adds `d ∩ Ann(d) = Coord(n, empty)` (exact under the Euclidean identification of 2.4; it decides the integrated end of a `Spanned` kernel, so `riesz` of a `V/Spanned` slot and of its image are decidable, S2 of the structure check). Intersection is exact on `Coord` only; `Coord ∩ Spanned` is refused unless the generator declares it, except the two exact cases `Coord(n, all) ∩ d = d` and `Coord(n, empty) ∩ d = Coord(n, empty)` (check 2 structure N03 F7), which decide the constructibility of `V/Spanned`. The name `Spanned` is chosen because `Declared` is already an `Origin` arm (`numerics/axis.py:265`).
- `Origin = FromMeasure | FromQuadrature | FromBasis | FromFrame | Declared` (existing, five arms, unchanged; the parent of a restricted axis is the `Restriction` identity field of `Axis`, 3.2, not an arm).
- `Testing = NODE | INTEGRAL`; `Kind`, `VarianceChart`, `Variance` as in S4.

### 2.4 Conventions, one address each

| convention | its one address |
|---|---|
| slot order | the order of `TensorSpace.factors`; `of_axes(a, b)` places `a` first; `FactoredMetric`'s entries follow it |
| order of the Kronecker application | `TensorSpace.flat_on(labels)` makes one call of `FactoredMetric._walk` (kept, `numerics/metric.py:696` region) over the formed blocks of `labels` in slot order; `_walk` is the one body that spells the walk, `flat_on` and `sharp_on` its only callers, and no other file spells a leg order |
| representative of a subquotient coordinate | the stored coordinates are on the axis's full index set; a member of `S/K` vanishes off `S` and its `K` coordinates are gauge; member equality is MODULO `K` (equal on the coordinates of `S` minus `K` for a `Coord` `K`); the invariant leg is the coset minimum, never a section (D10, D11, amendment A2) |
| the orientation of a face | the function `sigma_f = sgn(c_n)` on the angular index set (`Orientation.sign()`, L15); no factor or axis identity holds it, and a face's summand is identified by its label (`FaceSpec.key`, a `str`) |
| the half-range | the sign of the angular generator's cosine along the face normal (L12), computed in one function `numerics/tangential.py::half_range(quadrature, axis_index, *, sign=+1)`, returning a frozen `HalfRange(outflow, inflow, tangential)` (P2-8: it takes the quadrature and the axis index, never a bare array; six cutoffs join it, P2-7 and P2b-5) |
| the Euclidean identification of `R^I` with its dual | the canonical pairing `sum_i x_i y_i`; `Ann` is taken with respect to it |
| the data side | derived from the state side by L11; the state chart is DECLARED by the layout the scheme chooses (D16, U6) |
| the face area | `FaceSpec.measure` (contract 2), minted once by the adapter `trace_faces`; `SNSolver._face_area_of` is deleted (P2-11) |
| the labels of a direct sum | declared once, by the builder that mints the sum (P2-5): `bulk` and `trace` for the full field (constants in `numerics/direct_sum.py`), the coupled builders' own labels; `CoupledField` and the full-field composite hold the space they belong to and do not re-derive it |

---

## 3. The types

All modules are in `orpheus/numerics/` (layer `numerics`, phase `structure`; imports only `geometry/`, `memo/`, `values/` and lower `numerics/` modules; the layer gate `tests/gates/test_layer_imports.py` is unchanged) except `transport/four_spaces.py` (layer `transport`). Each class's literal declaration comment is given: it is the trailing comment on the line that ends the class header, `# concept: <id> | role: <role> | kind: <kind>[.<subkind>]` (grammar of `.claude/plans/ontology_w0_spec_c.md` 2.3; a missing or wrong comment reddens `tests/gates/test_concept_table.py`). "Refuses" means a `ValueError` (or `TypeError` for a wrong type) raised at construction with a message whose distinctive fragment is quoted; a gate greps that fragment (section 7). No new exception class is minted.

### 3.1 `numerics/declaration.py` (new, built in N01: the rows `Coord`, `Spanned`, `Ann`, `Declaration`) and `numerics/factor.py` (new, built in N03: the other rows; concepts `dual`, `subspace`, `factor`)

The declaration rows are built first (unit N01) because `DenseMetric` takes a `Declaration` as its `kernel`; the module path of those four rows is `numerics/declaration.py`, not `factor.py`. They are `ContentIdentity` classes and join the content-identity roster in N01 (P2-6; check 2 elegance N00-N02 E4: otherwise `test_s5_9_the_population_is_the_roster` reds at N01).

| type | declaration comment | fields | invariant asserted at construction | identity | verbs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Kind(Enum)` | `# concept: factor \| role: component \| kind: set` (N03; moved to concept `dual` at N07, SD-29) | `FUNCTION`, `MEASURE` | none (closed) | the member | `flip()` |
| `VarianceChart(Enum)` | `# concept: factor \| role: component \| kind: set` (N03; moved to concept `dual` at N07, SD-29) | `DENSITY`, `INTEGRATED` | none | the member | `flip()` |
| `Variance` (frozen dataclass) | `# concept: factor \| role: component \| kind: set` at N03 (SD-29), moved to `# concept: dual \| role: head \| kind: set` at N07 when `DualSpace` (the old head) is deleted | `kind: Kind`, `chart: VarianceChart` | none: all four values are legal values of the Klein set; legality against a form is the factor's (A0) | the pair | `dual()` (flips both), `riesz()` (flips the chart): the two involutions of O4 |
| `Coord` | `# concept: subspace \| role: part \| kind: set` | `n: int`, `indices: tuple[int, ...]` | `n >= 0`; `indices` sorted, unique, each in `[0, n)` ("indices must be sorted ascending and unique" is the fragment `AngularFaceTraceSpace` already pins at `tests/gates/numerics/test_angular_face_trace_space.py:962-991`; keep it) | `(n, indices)` | `dim`, `ann()` (the complement: the one verb) |
| `Spanned` | same | `n: int`, `key: tuple`, `dim: int` | `0 <= dim <= n`; `key` non-empty and hashable (a content key the generator owns, for example `("frame-gram-kernel", frame digest)`) | `(n, key, dim)` | `dim`, `ann()` |
| `Ann` | same | `n: int`, `of: Declaration` | `of.n == n`; `d.ann()` is the only door (`Coord.ann()` returns the `Coord` complement, `Spanned.ann()` an `Ann`, `Ann.ann()` its `of`), and `Ann.__post_init__` refuses an `of` that is not a `Spanned`, because a dataclass constructor cannot return another type; so a second spelling of one subspace is unspellable | `(n, of)` | `dim = n - dim(of)` |
| `Declaration` | named closed sum `Coord \| Spanned \| Ann` (an alias, not a class) | | | | `contains(a, b) -> bool` (raises where the sum cannot decide, SD-25, 2.3), `intersect(a, b) -> Declaration \| None` |
| `Subquotient` | `# concept: subspace \| role: component \| kind: space` at N03, promoted to `role: head` at N06 when `AngularFaceTraceSpace` (the old head) is deleted | `S: Declaration`, `K: Declaration` | same `n`; `K ⊆ S` is decided true (undecided is refused: "subquotient kernel is not declared inside its subspace") | normalised `(S, K)`: `(in ∪ tang)/tang` and `Sub(in)` are isomorphic and distinct (their duals differ, A2 B3) and compare unequal | `whole(n)`, `dual() -> Subquotient` (`(Ann K, Ann S)`), `dim` (`dim S - dim K`), `is_whole` |
| `Factor` | `# concept: factor \| role: head \| kind: space` | `axis: Axis`, `variance: Variance`, `sk: Subquotient` (every `Factor` construction states its variance; there is no default and no `Factor.on`, so a placeholder variance is unspellable, amendment A1) | (a) `sk.S.n == prod(axis.shape)`; (b) `sk.S` and `sk.K` are declarations on the flattened index set `R^prod(shape)` (row-major), for an axis of any rank (SD-40; `Axis.restricted` stays rank-1 only); (c) A0: `axis.form` is `NoForm` implies `variance.chart is DENSITY` iff `variance.kind is FUNCTION`; (d) constructibility (P2-1, S6): where the axis has a form, a DENSITY factor needs `S ∩ ker G ⊆ K` and an INTEGRATED factor needs its `riesz` image to be constructible; otherwise refused here with the message naming the kernel to quotient by (a whole `sk` on a singular form is refused), never at a later read | `("factor", axis.identity_key(), variance, sk)`; `==` and hash derive from it only | `dual()`, `riesz()` (total on every constructible formed factor, P2-1; refused with "riesz is not defined: " only on a formless axis, where A0 forbids a chart change), `stored_shape` (the axis's shape, S5), `dim` (`dim S - dim K`), `form -> HilbertMetric \| NoForm` (P2-4, amendment A4): `NoForm` when the axis form is `NoForm`; the axis form when `sk` is whole; on a DENSITY factor the coset minimum of the axis form on `S/K` (the Schur complement), stored on the axis's full index set with kernel `K ∪ (I minus S)` for a `Coord` pair and the generator's declared `Spanned` kernel for `V/Spanned`; on an INTEGRATED factor the inverse face built by the existing constructors (`DenseMetric.inverse_of`, `DualMetric`, the masked reciprocal), never a recomputed `1/w`, so the bytes are today's. The admission table of `(form arm, S arm, K arm)` (check 2 elegance N03 F2): (`Diag`, `Coord`, `Coord`) and (`Dense`, `Coord`, `Coord`) are induced; (`Dense`, whole, `Spanned` equal to the axis form's declared kernel) is the density partner of the frame head; (`Dense`, `S = Ann(Spanned)` with the `Spanned` equal to the axis form's declared kernel, `K` the empty `Coord`) is the integrated frame head itself, whose form is the inverse face below; (`Dense`, any other `Ann` `S`) is refused with "subquotient subspace is not the annihilator of the form's declared kernel" `[R]` the fragment is the gate's to pin; (`Dense`, whole, any other `Spanned`) is refused with "subquotient kernel is not the form's declared kernel"; (any form, a `Spanned` `S`) is refused with "a declared subspace has no enumerable coordinates"; no `NotImplementedError` is reachable, no `is_legal_riesz` (P2-1). The `sk` production of a frame head (P2-4): a frame head stores moments, so it is the INTEGRATED factor `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)` on `Ann K` (`sk = kernel.ann()/Coord(n, empty)`: `kernel.ann()` is `Ann(kernel)` on the DENSE verdict, where `kernel` is a `Spanned`, and the `Coord` complement on the DIAGONAL verdict, where `kernel` is a `Coord`; an `Ann` is built through `ann()` only, 3.1), the `riesz` image of the density factor `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` on `V/Spanned(kernel)` whose form is the Gram `G`; whole on a full-rank head. Its `Factor.form` is the inverse face that today's `_parseval_dressed` builds (`numerics/frame_faces.py:446-464`: `DenseMetric.inverse_of(G)` on the DENSE verdict, the masked reciprocal of `diag(G)` on the DIAGONAL verdict), so the bytes are today's |

Unspellable after this section: a factor with variance and no form where A0 is broken; a subquotient whose kernel escapes its subspace; two spellings of one quotient; a `riesz` on a singular slot that is not quotiented by its kernel; a formed factor on a singular form with no declared quotient (P2-1).

### 3.2 `numerics/axis.py` (edited)

`Axis` (frozen, `ContentIdentity`, concept `factor`, **`# concept: factor | role: weld | kind: space | welded: factor, fmetric`**, was head with the same weld; the head moves to `Factor` and the weld stays, because the axis still holds the origin, the index set and the form in one class, so the weld tag is the true reading, and its debt entry stays until W8a types the generators; P2b-3, SD-41, simulated in `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2b/p2b_3_simulate.out`: 0 new and 0 stale rows). Fields: `label: str` (non-empty), `shape: tuple[int, ...]` (rank >= 0, P2-2), `form: Form` (P2-4: the closed sum of three arms; derived, `init=False`, on EVERY arm: `Axis.form` is `origin.form`, so `Axis(..., form=...)` is a `TypeError` on all five arms and the defect #710 is unspellable; the `Declared` arm holds its form, `Declared(kind, form)` with `DiagonalMetric.counting(prod(shape))` as the default, and `FromQuadrature` holds the optional `face_axis`), `origin` (kw-only, `compare=False`), `restriction: Restriction | None` (kw-only, default `None`, IN the identity), derived `kind`, `support`, `nodes`, `layout`. `Restriction` (frozen, **`# concept: factor | role: part | kind: set`**) holds `parent_key` (the parent axis's `identity_key`) and `indices` (a sorted tuple of ints). Invariants: `form.validate_for(shape)` if present; `EnergyAxis` has `form = NoForm`. On a generator arm the form is read from the origin (`FromMeasure` and `FromQuadrature`: the measure's weights as `DiagonalMetric`; `FromBasis`: the continuum Gram where the family has one, else `NoForm`, SD-8; `FromFrame`: the Gram `G` of the basis under the measure, on the coefficient side (P2-4): a `DenseMetric` with the frame's declared kernel on the DENSE verdict and a `DiagonalMetric` of `diag(G)` (kernel the exact zero entries) on the DIAGONAL verdict of `frame.discrete_gram_structure`; today's head carries `G+` because it stores moments, so it is the INTEGRATED factor of 3.1 whose `Factor.form` is the inverse face today's code builds (`numerics/frame_faces.py:446-464`); answered by `FromFrame.form` in N03, the row having moved from N01 because `numerics/axis.py` is in N03's file set; `Declared`: the form it is handed). Identity as today with `form` content in place of `weights`, and `restriction`.

**Constructors (amendment A5, SD-21), all built in N03.** Read from `numerics/axis.py:461-640`: the existing `Axis.of_measure(measure, label)`; `Axis.of_quadrature(quadrature, label="angular", *, face_axis: int | None = None)` (with `face_axis` given, the constructor itself reads the quadrature's cosine along that axis, `quadrature.mean_axis_cosine(face_axis)` at `directional.py:299`, and the form is `DiagonalMetric(abs(cosine) * weights)`, exactly zero where the cosine is exactly zero; it takes no caller-supplied array, because an array that can disagree with the quadrature is the #710 defect again; the origin stays `FromQuadrature`; a `face_axis` outside `[0, 3)` is refused with the accessor's own error (`mean_axis_cosine` raises only there; `[M]` `check2/n03_probe_cosine.py`: on a rule of lower dimension an in-range axis returns all zeros, so a suppressed axis yields an all-kernel form by design, and the trace builder refuses a face whose cosine is identically zero, P2-8, 3.6)); `Axis.of_basis(basis)`; and the bare `Axis(label, shape, *, origin=FromFrame(frame))` or `Axis(label, shape, *, origin=Declared(kind, form))`. There is no `Axis.of(generator, label)`, no `FactorGenerator` and no `Axis.declared`: a generator-less one-factor space is `TensorSpace.declared` (3.4). Retired: the `weights=` constructor argument on every arm and the field `Axis.weights` (it is not kept as a property, amendment A5, so the readers re-point in N03: the production reads `numerics/space.py:1193` and `:1276`, the generic content encoder `values/content.py:346` and 48 test reads, SD-17), and the guard `ELEGANCE-DEBT[guard] #710` at `axis.py:569` (a passed weight or a passed form, on any arm, is a `TypeError`, G03d). New verb `Axis.restricted(indices) -> Axis`: a NEW axis of shape `(len(indices),)` with origin `Declared(BasisKind.NODAL, form)` (a kept `FromQuadrature` origin would re-derive a full-length form and nodes, which `numerics/axis.py:539-544` refuses), form the principal submatrix, whole `sk`, `restriction = Restriction(self.identity_key(), indices)`. Only a `DiagonalMetric` parent is admitted: a dense parent is refused as a declared edge, `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[restricted-dense] machinery: the only consumer is the face-angular axis, and orpheus/ may not compute a numerical kernel; ruling: check 2 elegance N03 F6; revisit: W8a`. Refused on a rank != 1 axis, on a `Modal` parent (`kind.unit_over(shape)`, `:524`), on an unsorted or repeated index set (fragment "sorted ascending and unique", the sentence `AngularFaceTraceSpace` pins at `tests/gates/numerics/test_angular_face_trace_space.py:962-991`) and on an empty set. The `case FromQuadrature` consumers of contract 1 see no quadrature on a half-range view. `EnergyAxis` (concept `factor`, **`# concept: factor | role: arm | kind: space`**, its present declaration, unchanged: P2b-3, SD-41): rank 1, `form = NoForm`, `Origin = Declared(NODAL)` fixed; `of_edges`, `synthetic` unchanged.

**Rank 0 (P2-2).** An axis may have index shape `()`: one point, no array axis. The face-spatial factor of a 1-D face is one: it contributes nothing to the stored shape (so a slot `(N, ng)` stays `(N, ng)`), carries the form `Diag(A_f)` as one weight (counting before the area unit), and is labelled `"spatial"` in every summand. This is the law the tree already states for a one-wide factor, `factor_tail` (`numerics/space.py:1740`: `V ⊗ R ≅ V`, so composing with it leaves the space and every buffer unchanged). What it needs: the constructor refuses rank 0 (`numerics/axis.py:534-538`, "an Axis needs rank >= 1") and is the one edit in `Axis` itself. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2/p2_2_rank0_behaviour.py` (run from the repository root): a 0-d `DiagonalMetric` constructs and validates for shape `()`, and a `FactoredMetric` with a `()` block between ranked blocks applies, inverts and reports rank 6 on a `(3, 2)` array, byte-equal to the manual slot-order product; `[M]` `amend2/p2_2_rank0.py`: the axis-level reads of rank already sum `len(ax.shape)` (`axis_marginal.py:466-467`, `space.py:700`) or concatenate shapes (`space.py:537`, `:1466`), so a `()` block adds nothing to them, and `np.prod(()) = 1.0` keeps the node-count check and `Subquotient.S.n == prod(axis.shape)` valid. `[R]` not run: an `Axis` of shape `()` through `layout`, `_axis_index`, `AxisRetractionOperator` (its `dims` is empty for it, `axis_marginal.py:466-467`) and `Factor`; that is an N06 opening measurement, and if one of them needs a special case beyond the constructor section 11 records it (SD-31). The radial boundary space's one-point corner factor (3.6) follows the same rule: index shape `()`, so its summand keeps shape `(ng,)`.

### 3.3 `numerics/metric.py` (edited; concept `fmetric`, head `HilbertMetric`)

- `NoForm` (a frozen field-less dataclass, so that it is encodable as `Axis` identity content; **`# concept: fmetric | role: component | kind: element`**; not a `HilbertMetric`): it has no `apply`, `inner_product`, `norm` or `flat`; each consumer matches on `Form` (2.3, the one definition) exhaustively.
- `DiagonalMetric`: new classmethod `counting(n)` (all-ones weights); refuses a negative weight at construction (`#706`, U3 default; fragment "negative weight"); reads kernel (the indices of exactly-zero weights, `Coord`) and signature `(n_+, n_0, 0)` exactly from the signs. Zero-weight handling unchanged.
- `DenseMetric(matrix, *, kernel: Declaration)`: `kernel` is required and is a compare field, so part of content identity (P2-6); it must be `Coord(n, ())` (full rank) or a `Spanned`, and a non-empty `Coord` is refused (fragment "kernel is not declared by a spanned subspace"), because the SVD drops the smallest singular directions and a named index could disagree with them (check 2 elegance N00-N02 E5); there is no default, so every one of the 18 test files and 63 `DenseMetric(` call sites in `tests/` names it, and the one production mint `DenseMetric.inverse_of` at `numerics/frame_faces.py:459` receives it from the frame; `[M]` AST `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/out/census_raw.json`: 0 production bare `DenseMetric(` calls, 18 test files with 63 sites). The inverse face is, in this exact arithmetic (D8): `u, s, vt = np.linalg.svd(g_sym, hermitian=True)`; zero exactly `kernel.dim` of the smallest singular values (`sinv = 1/s` for the kept ones, `0` for the dropped); `inverse = vt.T @ (sinv[:, None] * u.T)`. No `rcond`, `rtol` or `atol` keyword and no `pinv` call appears in the module (the symmetry admission `_DENSE_METRIC_SYMMETRY_RTOL = 1e-12` at `metric.py:264` is kept as an INPUT validity predicate and tagged `ELEGANCE-DEBT[guard] #634`, not a structure decision; the Penrose admission `_DENSE_METRIC_PENROSE_RTOL` retires and so does the supplied-inverse keyword `inverse_matrix=` as a PUBLIC keyword: `inverse_of(gram, *, kernel)` sets both faces in ONE private body (the SVD recombination as the first face and the Gram's exact symmetrisation as the second), and the bare constructor uses the same body, deriving the second face from the matrix, so there is no admission and no public supplied-inverse path (P2-6). The faces are named by role: `metric` (`.matrix`, G+) and `gram` (`inverse_matrix` today, the symmetrised Gram). `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n01_inverse_face.py`: a builder that derived the second face from `matrix` would get `(G+)+`, with `matrix` bit-equal on 14 of 14 dense frames and the second face bit-equal on 0 of 14 (maximum difference 4.4e-15 to 1.2e-14), so `inverse_of` must keep the exact Gram. `[M]` AST: `inverse_matrix=` is a keyword in 1 production file (`metric.py` itself) and 0 test files. N01 retires the inverse-face admission that the subject of issue #634 names (`#634` is open; the unit's opening comments on it, `[R]`); the symmetry admission stays under its tag. The fate of `tests/gates/numerics/test_axis_identity.py::test_I5c` (`:131`): its conclusion, that the supplied and the derived second faces make ONE space, survives because identity is `(matrix, kernel)` and the second face is derived data; its premise (the two second faces differ in round-off) holds by the measurement above; it is re-spelled at N01 with `kernel=form.kernel` on the rebuilt metric and at N04 on the typed space, its API (`_overlay_forms`, `_with_overlay`) being deleted there). `rank(shape) = (n - kernel.dim) * (size // n)` with `size` the product of `shape` (today's trailing factor, `metric.py:632-634`, read); the signature is `(n - kernel.dim, kernel.dim, 0)` by the declaration (S2, amendment A6); `kernel_basis` is the `eigh` split with the count from `kernel.dim` (no `rank` read from a cutoff). `[M]` `measure_inverse_bytes.md`: this recombination is bit-equal to today's `pinv(hermitian=True, rcond=1e-12)` on 21 of 21 distinct dense Grams that production builds (16 frame-built, 5 synthetic) in 101 gate files (3128 passed, 2 xfailed), and a one-ulp bump of `G[0,0]` changes the bytes on 20 of 21 (the one miss is a 3x3 rank-2 synthetic Gram the control does not test); sample, not the 806 test files. The 21 of 21 covers `inverse_of` only (`measure_inverse_bytes.md` (a)): the `metric` face (`.matrix`, G+) is bit-equal and the `gram` face is the exact symmetrisation, identical; the bare-constructor path at `metric.py:568`, which serves the 63 test sites, was never measured, and its byte comparison is N01's opening obligation `[R]`.
- `FactoredMetric`: entries per slot are of type `Form`: the energy block is `NoForm` and `_walk` skips it (today's `None`, on 32,672 of 32,680 hits `[M]`), and a `None` entry on a FORMED axis today means Euclidean and becomes `DiagonalMetric.counting(n)` (migration hazard, D5). `PositionedMetric` (Protocol) stays until N04 deletes the overlay (when the unit's AST finds no reader).
- `DualMetric`, `HilbertMetric`: unchanged. The kernel and signature are read on the form, once, not also on the axis (D7).
- The frame-owned tolerance (D9, U4): `numerics/frame_faces.py` owns ONE named constant `FRAME_GRAM_RANK_RTOL = 1e-12` with its tag `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[guard] machinery: an exactness-degree certificate on the quadrature (W8a); consumer: the harmonic head on under-resolved rules; ruling: D9/U4, orchestrator, 2026-11-23; revisit: W8a's exactness certificate` (the CI ledger gate `tests/gates/test_elegance_debt_is_tagged.py:142` requires `machinery:`, `ruling:` and `revisit:` within three lines, `[R]` read, not run). The frame computes `kernel.dim` as the count of its own Gram's singular values at or below `FRAME_GRAM_RANK_RTOL * s_max`, in exact one place, and declares it as `Spanned(n, ("frame-gram-kernel", content_digest(g_sym)), dim)` with `g_sym` the read-only symmetrised Gram (`content_digest(frame)` itself raises `ContentlessError`, because `measure.nodes` is writeable, `[M]` `check_structure_N00-N02.md` F5; the test helper names its key the same way). Where a theorem gives the rank (a rule exact to degree `2L` gives a full-rank Gram; Legendre division is rational) the frame declares it from the theorem and a gate checks it in exact arithmetic. `[M]` `measure_inverse_bytes.md`: every frame-built Gram has dropped singular values at most 2.3e-16 of the largest and kept at least 1.8e-2, 4 orders from the cutoff on both sides; the only near-edge Gram is synthetic (drops 5e-13 against 1e-12). Of the 16 frame Grams 4 are full rank by Vandermonde and 12 are under-resolved; 2 of those 12 (Legendre) have a theorem rank and 10 (spherical-harmonic or mirror-even) have their rank from the cutoff only, so the frame's named tolerance needs the under-resolved frames of `tests/gates/numerics/test_frame.py` (10 of the 12) as witnesses; the theorem-backed count is 6 of 16 `[R]` (the Oh-24 Legendre Gram's rank is inferred, not enumerated).
- The form constructor does NOT test `G K = 0` in floating point (`[M]` A2 B5: `|G K| = 3.3e-16`, 4 of 21 entries exactly zero); that check is the gate `test_dense_kernel_declared.py` with its tolerance in the gate (D9).
