Verbs and their refusals. `dual(on=L)`, `riesz(on=L)`: L4, L5; an unknown label raises `KeyError` naming the labels; `on=()` returns `self`. `riesz` raises "riesz is not defined: " when any slot of `L` is `NoForm` (an illegal formed slot cannot be constructed, P2-1) and when `L` cuts a form block. **One body for the metric verbs (amendment A5, L10):** `flat_on(labels)` makes one call of `FactoredMetric._walk` over the formed blocks of `labels` in slot order, one block at a time (2.4); `sharp_on(labels)` is its inverse (the masked divide `where(w != 0, x / w, 0)` for a diagonal form); `flat`, `sharp`, `apply_metric`, `apply_inverse_metric`, `integral` and `DualPairing.covector` are one call to them (`flat` is `flat_on(all labels)`, `sharp` is `sharp_on(all labels)`, `apply_metric` is `flat_on(formed labels)`, `apply_inverse_metric` is `sharp_on(formed labels)`), and an `ast` gate asserts the delegation (G04l, G09f). From N11, `inner_product` and `norm` raise "no partial form: complete the formless slots with euclidean()" whenever any block is `NoForm`, and whole-space `flat` and `sharp` refuse a `NoForm` block. Between N04 and N11 (the energy window) a `NoForm` block is treated exactly as today's `FactoredMetric` treats its `None` entry: skipped by `flat_on` and `sharp_on`, counting in `inner_product` and `norm`; these transitional behaviours carry the tag `ELEGANCE-DEBT[energy-absent] N11`. From N11 `flat_on(L)` and `sharp_on(L)` refuse a `NoForm` label in `L`, and `apply_metric` and `apply_inverse_metric` are PERMANENT partial verbs that select the formed labels themselves, with no tag (check 2 structure N11 F6). The operator ends that cross the window (`RieszLowerOperator.codomain` is `space.dual()`, under which the energy slot flips kind) carry `ELEGANCE-DEBT[energy-absent-adjoint] W2 (the adjoint from the pairing)`, outside G11d's predicate (check 2 elegance N11 E4). `EnergyAxis.form` is `NoForm` from N03, as a type; from N04 production mints the energy factor of the flux `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`, legal under A0 because the energy form is `NoForm`, so the variance the window carries is the one R14 declares. `euclidean()` returns a DIFFERENT space (its identity differs) whose `NoForm` forms are replaced by `DiagonalMetric.counting(n)` (energy only: the angular and spatial forms are kept). `counting()` is the second completion (every form replaced by counting, formed or not). Two completions, because `[M]` `measure_pairings.md` section 2: today's 15 `np.linalg.norm` sites are COORDINATE norms (`euclidean()` moves `iteration.py:428` on 0 of 47,613 hits to bit equality, a median relative 0.65), so the 15 numpy norm sites are NOT re-pointed in W1 (SD-4, C3); the two production `space.norm` sites (`diffusion/solver.py:645`, `:646`) read `space.euclidean().norm(e_V(x))` (S13), equal to today because today's energy entry is `None` (counting) `[M]` 6 of 6 hits bit-equal on the concatenated sum.

**The stored field `name` (P2-14).** Today `name` is a stored field of the base (`numerics/space.py:308-344`). On the two arms it is replaced by a derived display name: a `TensorSpace`'s joins its factor labels with the shape and the identity digest (as the readable name `energy(2,)#5154e7983e533859` does today), a `DirectSumSpace`'s joins its summand labels. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2/p2_14_name_readers.py` (AST; control: the known reader `kernel_projection_gauge.py:171`, found): 28 `.name` reads on a receiver whose last identifier matches space, domain, codomain, trace, bulk or primal, in 13 production files (`orpheus/geometry/manifold.py`, `numerics/basis/descent.py`, `numerics/basis/legendre_basis.py`, `numerics/coupled_system.py`, `numerics/field.py`, `numerics/frame.py`, `numerics/frame_faces.py`, `numerics/generating_measure.py`, `numerics/kernel_projection_gauge.py`, `numerics/measure.py`, `numerics/space.py`, `sn/boundary/realized_operators.py`, `transport/frames/harmonic_frame.py`) and 26 in 12 test files; an upper bound by receiver name, since some receivers are not spaces (a manifold, a basis). The runtime spy that settles the real readers is N07's opening obligation.

**The verbs the bit-identity instrument reads (P2-15).** The N00 harness reads spaces only through `apply_metric`, `apply_inverse_metric`, `metric_diagonal` and `n_coordinates`, which every unit keeps (`metric_diagonal` is kept, above), and reaches the spaces through the problem accessors, never by class. It builds its own population explicitly: the frame families of `measure_inverse_bytes.md` (b) (at least 21 distinct dense Grams), the SN slab, a 2-D box, the sphere, the cylinder and the diffusion slab, with `ng` 1, 2 and 3. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n00_dense_spy.py` (`check_structure_N00-N02.md` F2): on the three fixture files the first draft named, `DenseMetric.inverse_of` is called 0 times and 0 dense Grams are built (positive control: `tests/gates/numerics/test_dense_metric.py` counts 3 calls and 3 Grams), so harness (c) cannot be read from them. The unit plan 6.0a says which unit re-points a snapshot's getter when it deletes the class the getter returned.

Carrier hook (D27): ONE `compare=False` field `carrier` on `TensorSpace` and on `DirectSumSpace`, tagged `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[member-class] machinery: member classes follow their space (W3)`; `dual_zeros_factory` and `zeros_factory` retire into it as `CarrierSpec(zeros, dual_zeros)`, a frozen pair of factories whose `dual()` swaps the two (so the dual of a space with a carrier has the swapped carrier, as `CoupledSpace._make_dual` does today, `coupled_system.py:547-558`). No gate asserts equality on it. Two direct sums that are equal but carry different carriers are a known defect already routed to W3 (`true_ends.md` A3); a gate asserts the carrier is not part of identity (`test_space_identity_census.py` keeps its relation test).

### 3.4a The production space builders and the variance each factor is minted with (P2-4)

One row per production call that builds a space (check 2 structure N04: 11 calls in 6 files, 4 of them inside `numerics/space.py`; the first draft's "15 calls in 7 files" counted two axis mints, `transport/spatial/scheme.py:1799` and `transport/mesh/material_mesh.py:599`, which build no space). `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n04_probe1.py` (AST over `orpheus/`, derivations excluded) lists the calls; the variance column is the layout's declaration (D16), and `[R]` (ruled, P2b-9, SD-35) for `test_space`, `measure_space` and the function role of `bulk_space`. Every `Factor` is built with its variance stated; `sk` is whole unless the row says otherwise (P2-1).

| call | space | factors in slot order: variance, `sk`, form | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `transport/phase_spaces.py:60` `angular_bulk_space` | `<angular, energy, spatial>` | angular `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` from `Axis.of_quadrature(q)` with form `Diag(w)`; energy `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`, `NoForm`; spatial `(MEASURE, DENSITY)`, form `Diag(V_cell)`; on a widened iterate the cell-moment factor `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` under `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[cell-moment] W3` | re-spells as `TensorSpace.of_axes(angular_factor, *mesh.bulk_space.factors)`, so energy and spatial are the bulk space's own factors; the flux row of 7.13 |
| `homogeneous/solver.py:214` | `<energy, spatial>` of the 0-D baseline | energy `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`; spatial `Axis("spatial", (1,), origin=Declared(NODAL, counting))`, `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` | the flux row fits `[R]` |
| `transport/mesh/material_mesh.py:612` `MaterialMesh.bulk_space` | `<energy, spatial>` | energy `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`; spatial `(MEASURE, DENSITY)`, form `Diag(volumes)` (from `volume_measure` on a rank-1 mesh; `Declared(NODAL, Diag(volumes))` on the rank-d arm) | the flux role. The function-valued role of the same mesh (cross-section fields read it as "the scalar-bulk function space") is DERIVED, `bulk_space.dual().riesz(on={"spatial"})`, which is the importance row `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` on both slots, never a second mint (SD-35(b), ruled, P2b-9) |
| `transport/frames/harmonic_frame.py:493` `moment_space_on` | `basis_space(frame) * <the angular bulk space's factors after the first>` | head: the row of `frame_faces.py:448`; energy `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`; spatial `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` | the moment layout's angular slot is `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)` because the head stores moments (D16: the layout declares it; G08b) |
| `numerics/frame_faces.py:448` `basis_space` (`_parseval_dressed`) | one factor on `Axis(origin=FromFrame(frame))` | `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`, `sk = kernel.ann()/Coord(n, empty)` on a rank-deficient head and whole on a full-rank head; `Axis.form` is the Gram `G`; `Factor.form` is the inverse face today's code builds (DENSE verdict: `DenseMetric.inverse_of(G, kernel=)`; DIAGONAL verdict: the masked reciprocal of `diag(G)`) | `_dressed` (`:356-369`), today's one production writer of the overlay (its `replace(..., metric=FactoredMetric(...))` at `:356` and the weights write at `:363`), retires with the overlay at N04 |
| `numerics/frame_faces.py:481` `test_space` | `frame.test is frame.basis`: the same integrated factor as the row above, so `test_space(frame) is basis_space(frame)` holds as today (the analysis lands in moments); a distinct test basis: `Factor(Axis.of_basis(frame.test), (MEASURE, INTEGRATED), whole)` | | SD-35(a), ruled (P2b-9): `test_space(frame)` is where the analysis lands, the moments, so it is the integrated factor; the `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` space on `V/K` is `test_space(frame).dual()`, which the moment-layout pair derives and does not mint; making `test_space` itself that space is not taken, because it would change the bytes of `apply_metric` on this space (G instead of G+) |
| `numerics/frame_faces.py:496` `measure_space` | one factor, `of_measure(frame.measure, label, Variance(MEASURE, DENSITY))` | `(MEASURE, DENSITY)`, whole, form `Diag(w)` | on a directional rule it IS the SN angular factor, as today |
| `numerics/space.py:463`, `:477` (`of_measure`, `of_basis`) | constructors | take the variance as an argument | no mint |
| `numerics/space.py:661`, `:669` (`without_axis`, `with_axis`) | constructors | `without_axis(label)` returns the other factors unchanged; `with_axis(factor)` takes a `Factor` and appends it last | no mint (3.4) |

The name-built mints are SD-27's. The axis-only mints (`transport/spatial/scheme.py:1799` `Axis.of_basis`, `transport/mesh/material_mesh.py:599` `SpaceFactorAxis.of_measure`) are wrapped by the rows above and carry no variance of their own.

### 3.5 `numerics/direct_sum.py` (new; concept `dsum`)

`DirectSumSpace(FunctionSpace)` (**`# concept: dsum | role: head | kind: space`**, and from N07 `concept: space | role: arm`, 8.1). Fields: `summands: tuple[tuple[str, FunctionSpace], ...]`, `layout: CoproductLayout` (derived, `init=False`), `carrier` (`compare=False`). Invariants: at least one summand; labels non-empty and pairwise distinct at each level; `layout.sizes == tuple(s.n_coordinates for _, s in summands)`. Identity: `("dsum", ((label, summand.identity_key()), ...))`, so two sums over isomorphic summands with different labels are unequal. Verbs: `of(**summands)`, `of_pairs(pairs, *, carrier=None)` (the carrier is keyword-only on this door alone, so no summand label can collide with it), `summand(label)`, `labels`, `leaf(*path)` (nested addressing), `slice_of(label)`, `pack(parts)`, `unpack(flat)`, `with_summand(label, space)`, `dual()` and `riesz()` summand-wise (the label-addressed `dual(on=)` and `riesz(on=)` of L6 exist from N05, built on `TensorSpace` and `Factor`: the first draft's two-step typing is deleted, SD-5; gate G05c), `partition_by(label=None)` (the summand partition: one part per summand, derived from `layout`), `rank`, `kernel_basis` (block-diagonal). The metric is block diagonal; no `axes`. A summand that is a sum nests (the production shape `CoupledSpace((FullFieldSpace, member))`, `sn/coupled_system.py:598`, `:657`). Offsets are computed once by `CoproductLayout` (L13).

**Summand labels (P2-5).** The labels of a direct sum are declared once, by the builder that mints it: `bulk` and `trace` for the full field (constants in `numerics/direct_sum.py`, with the one parser `bulk_and_trace(space, *, owner)` that replaces the four `isinstance(.., FullFieldSpace)` doors of `isotropic_transfer.py`, `lift.py`, `multiplication_operator.py` and `angular_lift.py`; its refusal keeps the fragment "composite DirectSumSpace"), and the coupled builders' own labels at `sn/coupled_system.py:598,:657` (`[R]` the names are the builder's choice). `CoupledField` (`numerics/coupled_system.py:313`) and the full-field composite (`transport/full_field.py:303`, kept until W3) hold the space they belong to as a field instead of re-deriving it positionally (`[M]` `check_elegance_N05.md` V1, `check_structure_N05.md` F1: both derive it label-less today), so `x.space == V` holds for a restricted coupled field; posing unit 4 item 3 ("`CoupledField` gains labels") lands in N05, and the offset loop of `CoupledField.from_flat` (`:429`) is re-pointed to the space's layout in the same unit. `SystemRestrictionOperator`'s codomain takes the parent's labels through `of_pairs`.

**What replaces the members of the two retired sums (check 2 elegance N05 V2, V4; structure N05 F3).** `interior_space` becomes `summand("bulk")`; `trace_space` becomes `summand("trace")`; `systems` becomes `tuple(s for _, s in summands)`; `n_systems` becomes `len(labels)`; `zeros()` and `dual_zeros()` become `carrier.zeros()` and `dual().carrier.zeros()`; `metric_diagonal` is the concatenation, `None` if any summand returns `None`; `shape` is `(n_coordinates,)`; `act_on_direct_sum_coordinates` (`numerics/space.py:1612-1643`) retires into `pack` and `unpack`. The metric verbs of a sum (`flat_on`, `sharp_on`, `apply_metric`, `apply_inverse_metric`, `inner_product`, `norm`, `euclidean()`, `counting()`, `metric_diagonal`) act summand-wise through `CoproductLayout` in label order, as `pack(summand.verb(part))`, each in the two charts that today's classes accept (a composite carrier rebuilt through `_recombine`, and flat coordinates with the trailing payload kept, `full_field_space.py:330-344`); the base's `_form_blocks()` is a partition of SLOT labels and has no meaning on a sum, so the sum overrides it, and the delegation gate G04l covers the sum. A flip addressed by `on=` to a label, or a whole `riesz()`, on a sum with a legacy-leaf summand is refused ("a legacy leaf holds no factor labels", SD-22 widened); an unknown label raises `KeyError` as on a tensor space and an illegal flip raises `ValueError`. `[M]` `check_structure_N05.md` F3 (attribute AST plus reading): readers of the retired members outside N05's file list are `sn/operators/loss_kernel_gauge.py:897,:944,:973,:1033`, `diffusion/solver.py:348` and `sn/splitting.py:394`.

`numerics/coproduct_layout.py` (the file `numerics/face_layout.py` renamed, git rename): `CoproductLayout` (**`# concept: layout | role: arm | kind: set`**, the class `FaceLayout[K]` renamed, its generic key kept) and `LayoutBlock` (**`# concept: layout | role: part | kind: set`**, `FaceSlot` renamed); N05 is a rename plus new members: `FaceLayout` has no `unpack`, `sizes` or `offsets` today (`[M]` `check_structure_N05.md` F7, an `ast` read of `face_layout.py`), and `pack` allocates `np.zeros(total_size)` with no payload axis, so `CoproductLayout` gains `unpack`, `sizes` and `offsets` and payload support in `pack` and `unpack` (the metric verbs of a sum need it), and the field `faces` is renamed `blocks`; `from_named_shapes` and `structure` are unchanged in behaviour at N05, so no trace identity moves there. `structure` includes the slot shapes from N06, when the trace classes are replaced (#684: a transposed slot changes identity; closed by the direct-sum identity, which holds the summand shapes). The messages `gap or overlap`, `face_arrays keys` and `at least one member space` stay verbatim (pinned, `measure_callsites.md` section 2: 15 of 30 raise/warn messages naming a retiring class have a real pin on the sentence after the class name).

### 3.6 `numerics/trace.py` (new; concept `trace`) and the radial builders

The head of `trace` is the MODULE, whose listed functions define the concept (the form ruling O36 admits only on a row whose ruling admits it; this is U8's default, section 10; the fallback if refused is the class `TraceBuilder` holding the same functions as classmethods).
- `FaceSpec` (Protocol, **`# concept: trace | role: interface | kind: space`**): the five attributes of contract 2 (section 4); `key` is a `str`.
- `Orientation` (frozen dataclass, **`# concept: trace | role: part | kind: set`**): `cosines: NDArray` (outward cosines per ordinate), derived `half: HalfRange` and `outflow`, `inflow`, `tangential` (the `Coord`s of `half`, by L12: exact sign tests; one type, so G06g compares with `==`) and `sign() -> NDArray` (`+1` on `outflow`, `-1` on `inflow`, `0` on `tangential`: the function `sigma_f = sgn(c_n)` on the angular index set, an element of the importance-side function space, amendment A3). Invariants parsed at construction: `cosines` finite and one-dimensional; the three sets are disjoint and cover `range(len(cosines))`. Identity: `(cosines bytes)`. `Orientation.of(face, quadrature)` is the one named constructor (`half_range(quadrature, face.axis_index, sign=face.sign)`; the product `sign * cosine` is written inside `half_range` alone, P2-8, P2-9). A frozen dataclass with a `cosines` field also exposes `Orientation(cosines=...)`; that a production call builds an `Orientation` only through `Orientation.of` is an `ast` assertion owed at N06's opening `[R]`, not a property of the class. The trace's holder, the problem, keeps `faces: tuple[FaceSpec, ...]` and the orientations by face key, `Mapping[str, Orientation]` built once (`[R]` the holder), so a consumer that holds a trace and no mesh reaches them there. `Orientation.partner(face_test)` builds `sigma_f ⊗ 1_E ⊗ 1_X` as a member of the importance-side face space (N10). It is a field of NO factor or axis identity: a face's summand is identified by its label (the face key `FaceSpec.key`, a `str`), so on a slab `outflow(xmin)` and `inflow(xmax)` are one angular factor in two differently labelled summands, which is correct (gate G06d).
- `half_range(quadrature, axis_index, *, sign=+1) -> HalfRange` in `numerics/tangential.py` (N02; P2-8): it takes the quadrature and the axis index (the same rule as `Axis.of_quadrature(face_axis=)`: the caller supplies no cosine array), reads `sign * quadrature.mean_axis_cosine(axis_index)`, refuses non-finite cosines, and returns a frozen `HalfRange(outflow, inflow, tangential)` of `Coord`s whose constructor asserts that they are disjoint and cover the ordinates; `mesh.axis.face_outflow_ordinates` and `Orientation` call it, and six cutoffs join it (P2-7's four: `TANGENTIAL_EPS`, `_OUTFLOW_COSINE_TOL`, `_TANGENTIAL_MU`, `_MU_DIRECTION_EPS`; and, P2b-5, `sn/sweep/cache.py:355`'s literal `1e-15` and `_OCTANT_SIGN_EPS` of `numerics/quadrature/directional.py:109`, `[R]` until N02's opening read confirms that each decides the sign or the zero of a direction cosine). The kernel `K` of the face-angular factor is NOT taken from it: the builder reads `K` from the face-angular form's kernel (`Coord`, read exactly from the zero weights of `Diag(abs(cos) * w)`), the one source of the tangential set for the quotient; the two coincide on every shipped rule (`[M]` `zero_weight_census.py`: 0 of 17 rules carry an exactly zero weight, 51 rule-and-axis pairs) and a rule with a zero weight on a non-tangential ordinate is the case that tells them apart (G06g).
- `trace_space(faces, quadrature, energy, *, face_moments=None) -> DirectSumSpace` (P2-3: `face_moments` is the scheme's face basis, the trace on a face of `DiscretizationSchemeBase.cell_basis(ndim)`, whose dimension is `face_moment_count(ndim) = p^(d-1)`, `transport/spatial/scheme.py:1124`, `:1150`; `None` on a slopeless closure and on every 1-D face): one summand per face, labelled by `face.key`, each the tensor space `<angular_f, energy, spatial_f>` and, where the scheme has a face basis, a fourth factor `moments_f`, so today's slot `(N, ng, *face_shape, *moment_tail)` (`transport/phase_spaces.py:66-78`) is kept (D20's three-factor wording is amended by P2-3: a summand is angular, energy, spatial, and the moment factor where the scheme has one): `angular_f = Factor(Axis.of_quadrature(quadrature, "angular", face_axis=face.axis_index), Variance(MEASURE, DENSITY), V/K)` with `V = Coord(n, all)` and `K` the kernel of the form `Diag(abs(cos_n) * w_n)` (3.2, one handle for the one generator: the earlier signature took two); `energy` is the bulk energy factor `Variance(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`; `spatial_f` is labelled `"spatial"` in every summand (the summand's label carries the face) and has index shape `()` on a 1-D face, a one-point factor that adds no array axis, so `(N, ng)` stays `(N, ng)` (P2-2, 3.2), and the face-cell shape on a multi-D mesh: `Axis("spatial", cell_shape, origin=Declared(NODAL, form))` with `Variance(MEASURE, DENSITY)`, whose form is counting until N10 and `Diag(A_f)` after (R9); the face-moment factor `moments_f` is `Variance(MEASURE, DENSITY)` with the counting form, under the tag `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[cell-moment] machinery: the chart of in-cell and face moments is the cell-moment factor's question; ruling: P2-3; revisit: W3 (the cell-moment factor)`. The variances are the R14 flux row (7.13), minted literally once; the trace is built once, on `TensorSpace` and `Factor`, with `sk = V/K` (the first draft's two-step typing is deleted, SD-5). The storage is the FULL ordinate tier, so flat dimension and every stored array are unchanged (D21: `[M]` `numerics/space` m2 2.1: trace shape `(128,)`, rank 64, kernel 64 on `Quadrature.product(4,4)` with 4 faces of 2 face cells). `trace_space` returns a plain `DirectSumSpace` (no trace subclass, D20: `[R]` a subclass compares equal to a plain sum, one value one type). The views, read from `AngularTraceSpace._face_spaces` (`angular_trace_space.py:598-646`), are functions of `numerics/trace.py` that take the orientation, because the space holds none (P2-9): `full(summand)` is the summand itself (the `V/K` factor on the full axis); `outflow(summand, orientation)` and `inflow(summand, orientation)` are `TensorSpace`s whose angular axis is the face-angular axis `.restricted(orientation.outflow)` (resp. `.inflow`) with whole `sk` and the factor's own variance, shapes `(|indices|, ...)` exactly as today's tiers (`(4, 2)` on `Quadrature.product(4, 4)`, `[M]` m2 2.1); `current(face)` is `summand.without_axis("angular")` (`TensorSpace.without_axis(label)` drops the factor of that label, as today's `without_axis` does at `space.py:649` for an axis-built space `[R]` read, not run): the face summand with its angular slot contracted, so its factors are the summand's own energy and spatial factors with their variances unchanged (`MEASURE, INTEGRATED` on energy, the spatial factor as minted); it is not a name-built mint (today's `current_space`, a plain leaf with no angular slot).
- **What the trace builder refuses (P2-8, P2-9).** A face whose cosine is identically zero (a suppressed axis: `mean_axis_cosine(1)` on a 1-D rule is all zeros, `[M]` `check2/n03_probe_cosine.py`) is refused with the fragment "requires genuine" kept, which `tests/gates/sn/operators/test_sn_boundary_realizer.py:339` and `test_lambertian_chain.py:651` pin as "requires genuine mu_z" and `build_omega_dot_n` raises today (`angular_trace_space.py:270-278`). A face whose factor has `K` meeting a half (L16: a rule with a zero weight on a non-tangential ordinate) is refused with the fragment "K meets a half-range" `[R]`.
- **What the deleted members become (check 2 N06 elegance F2, structure 3).** `face_names` becomes `FaceSpec.key` over the problem's `faces`; `outflow_indices_for_face` and `inflow_indices_for_face` become `Orientation.of(face, quadrature).outflow` and `.inflow` (`Coord`s); `omega_dot_n` becomes `Orientation.cosines`; `partial_current_metric` becomes the face summand's flat form (`metric_diagonal` of the summand); `outflow_restriction` and `inflow_restriction` become `TraceRestrictionOperator` on `outflow(summand, orientation)` and `inflow(summand, orientation)`, the per-sweep cache of `_face_restrictions` (`angular_trace_space.py:522-566`) keeping its place; `to_local` with its ERR-045 refusal (`sn/boundary/realized_operators.py:198`) retires, and its pinned sentence "are not ordinates of" with it (no successor holds `ordinate_indices`; the unit's opening decides); `slot_view`, `levels`, `ng`, `nx` of the radial classes become `summand(label)` and the builder's arguments. `[M]` `check_structure_N06.md` 3: four production files outside N06's 22 name members only: `transport/fields/angular_boundary_flux.py:158-160`, `sn/loss_representation/__init__.py`, `sn/loss_representation/sweep_schedule.py:264`, `sn/operators/streaming.py:910,:1033`.
- **The net current and the face area have one spelling each (P2-11).** `AngularBoundaryFlux.net_current(face)` becomes the partial pairing with the orientation's sign over the angular slot (codomain `current(summand)`), and the leakage of N10 is built on it; there is no second spelling. The face-cell area has one source, the face's `measure` of contract 2 (section 4): the adapter `trace_faces` owns both arms of today's `SNSolver._face_area_of` (the 1-D `areas[0|-1]`, and for d >= 2 the ascending-axis outer product of the transverse widths, `sn/solver.py:1802-1807`), `_face_area_of` is deleted, and the tests that monkeypatch it by name migrate (`tests/gates/sn/eigenvalue/test_keff_estimator_gate.py:288-346`, `:475-494`). `[M]` `check_structure_N10.md` F1 (`n10_probe3.py`): `MaterialMesh.areas` raises `AttributeError` on the 2-D and 3-D boxes, so the adapter cannot read it there. The #251 refusal of `sn/solver.py:1745-1755` (a face-moment tail beyond moment 0) stays ahead of the pairing as the declared edge of P2-3, and the refusal of the 2-D scalar trace at `transport/phase_spaces.py:92-112` (`SCOPE-BOUNDARY[guard]` #685) stays where it is.
- `hemispheric_trace(faces, energy) -> DirectSumSpace`: the diffusion (scalar) trace, built by the same builder shape (`_face_summand`) with its OWN two-point angular axis (`outflow = {0}`, `inflow = {1}`, form `Diag(1)`, `Variance(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`: its stored coordinates are partial currents) and `spatial_f` (labelled `"spatial"`, index shape `()` on the 1-D face) with form `Diag(A_f)` (already carried today, `[M]` `measure_trace_metric.md` (a): `ScalarTraceSpace` carries `A_f` on 3 of 3 diffusion fixtures). It is NOT a pushforward of the angular trace (SD-3, amendment A8: `[M]` the pushforward `m_h` of `abs(mu) w` is 0.5213 for GL4 slab and sphere, 0.5058 for slab GL8, 3.628 for the cylinder, so `A_f` equals it only if `M = 4` and the rule is exact, and the project's `M` is 2 or 4 pi, ratios 1/2 and pi). W1 states no pushforward law and chooses no normalisation; the identification of the two traces (`A_f m_h` or `A_f / m_h`) is a recorded input of W7 (section 9).
- The radial characteristic spaces (`RadialCharacteristicInteriorSpace`, `RadialCharacteristicBoundarySpace`, their base `_RadialCharacteristicSubSpace`) are not retired as a concept by W1 (amendment A7, SD-11): they are re-spelled, in N06, as two builder functions in `numerics/spaces/radial_characteristic_space.py` (the walk helpers `_validate_ray_space_inputs`, `_radial_characteristic_legs` stay), listed under concept `rayspace` (W4's), not under `trace`. `radial_interior_space(levels, *, ng, nx, cell_volumes) -> DirectSumSpace` returns one summand per `(level, sign)` leg of `_radial_characteristic_legs`, in the walk's order, labelled by the `str` `f"{level}{'+' if sign > 0 else '-'}"` (the successor of the tuple key that `slot_view` takes today, `[R]`), each the tensor space `<energy, cells>` of shape `(ng, nx)` (energy `Variance(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`, cells `Variance(MEASURE, DENSITY)` with form `Diag(V_cell)`, the `G_sd` state gauge, `[R]` the variances are the flux-like state reading and not a measured fact); `radial_boundary_space(levels, *, ng, nx, cell_volumes) -> DirectSumSpace` returns one summand per leg, each `<energy, corner>` of shape `(ng,)` with a one-point corner factor of index shape `()` (P2-2: it adds no array axis, so the shape stays `(ng,)`) whose form is `Diag(V(R))`, `V(R) = cell_volumes[-1]` (the outer cell volume, `[M]` `measure_trace_metric.md` (a): no `|mu|` and no `A_f`, varying with nx from 29.3 to 5.9 while `m_h` stays 0.5213). The values are unchanged (gate G06i); the legs are not faces (no normal, no area) so they do not use `FaceSpec`; `V(R)` is a STATE GAUGE of the radial seed, not a trace metric (SD-3).
- Retired: `AngularTraceSpace`, `AngularFaceTraceSpace` (including `to_local`; `current_space` becomes `current(face)` above), `ScalarTraceSpace`, the three radial characteristic classes, and the six half-range cutoffs `TANGENTIAL_EPS`, `_OUTFLOW_COSINE_TOL`, `_TANGENTIAL_MU`, `_MU_DIRECTION_EPS`, `_OCTANT_SIGN_EPS` and the literal at `sn/sweep/cache.py:355` (N02, P2-7 and P2b-5; the last two `[R]` until N02's opening read). The half-range views are the restricted-axis tensor spaces described under `trace_space`: today's outflow and inflow tiers are restricted axes (their arrays keep their shape), today's full tier is the `V/K` factor on the full axis, and today's current tier has no angular factor (`[M]` the reading of the four producers, 6.7).

### 3.7 `numerics/pairing.py` (new; concept `pairing`)

`DualPairing` (frozen, **`# concept: pairing | role: head | kind: element.covector`**; an element of `(left (x) right)*`, O39). Fields: `left: FunctionSpace`, `right: FunctionSpace`, derived `legs`, a tree that mirrors the summand tree whose leaves are tuples of `Leg`s (a closed sum `Canonical | FormLeg(form)`), one per factor in slot order; the value on a direct sum is the sum over the summands, in summand order, of the per-summand kernel, and a one-summand sum is byte-equal to its summand (check 2 structure N09 F5); `FormLeg.form` is `Factor.form`, the induced form, so the form leg of a `V/K` factor does not depend on the representative (check 2 structure N09 F3). Invariants parsed at construction: the two spaces have the same label sequence (recursively for direct sums), and the same axis identity per label (the stored shape is the axis's shape, so it is equal by construction under amendment A2); per factor the kinds are opposite ("pairing of two measures" / "pairing of two functions"); per factor the subquotients match: the canonical leg requires `right.sk == left.sk.dual()`, the form leg requires `right.sk == left.sk`, and anything else is refused ("pairing of mismatched subquotients"); per block both `INTEGRATED` is refused ("pairing of two integrated factors" is the R15 fragment, naming the label). The checks run per factor in the order kinds, charts, subquotients, so `(source, source)` and `(flux, source)` are refused by the kind check ("pairing of two measures") and R15 is reached only on opposite kinds that are both integrated, `(response, source)` on the spatial slot (check 2 structure N09 F2). Verbs: `__call__(x, y) -> float` (the full contraction, `covector(x).evaluate(y)`: one body, and `Covector.evaluate` stays the canonical contraction; `x` in `left`, `y` in `right`; a typed member of another space is refused, and a bare array is accepted when its shape equals the side's stored shape, fragment "operand shape", check 2 elegance N09 E6), `covector(x) -> Covector` (the partial application: `left.flat_on(labels whose leg is a form leg)(x)` for the form part and the canonical contraction for the rest, so it is not a second spelling of `flat`; check 2 structure N09 F6), `legs_of(label)` (what W2 reads to build the per-factor Riesz legs), `swap()`. Positional parameters are `(left, right)` so that `tests/gates/_true_ends.py::pairing_of` is unedited by W1 except for the re-spelling of D18. The contraction kernel is `float(np.sum(leg(x) * y))`, bit-equal on HP3 (1160 of 1160 hits), HP4 (1093 of 1093), HP5 (409 of 409: a commutative product by `A_f` of the already-contracted current, `measure_pairings.md:42`; the full contraction over the angular slot that N10 builds is not covered by that measurement, `[M]` `check2/n10_probe2.py`, a synthetic probe with `A_f = 1`, reads different bytes on 1163 of 2000 faces, so the slab identity of the leakage is `[R]` until N10 re-measures it), HP10 (30 of 30) and on 22 of 33 hits of HP6 (the other 11 within 1.8e-16, `tensordot` against `np.sum`) `[M]` `measure_pairings.md` section 1. `adjoint(A)` is W2's. `InnerProductFunctional` (`numerics/functional.py:186`) is NOT retired by W1 (P2b-8, which corrects P2-14): it stays under `ELEGANCE-DEBT[retraction] W2 (the one-axis retraction family)` and loses only its role as head of the concept `pairing`, which becomes `DualPairing` at N09. N09 re-declares it `# concept: pairing | role: weld | kind: element.covector | welded: pairing, natural, multiplier` and changes nothing else of it. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2b/p2b_8_simulate.out` (the tool's own checks over the tip with a stand-in `DualPairing` head added in memory; control: the tip reads 0 new and 0 stale): this declaration reads 0 new and 0 stale rows, both of its debt entries (`weld`, `covector_off_lineage`) stay pinned, and leaving the declaration as it is reads `concept 'pairing': 2 heads (InnerProductFunctional, DualPairing)`. Reason `[M]`: `RankOneOperator.is_adjointable` and `.apply_transpose` decide the fission transpose by `isinstance(self.functional, InnerProductFunctional)` (`numerics/elementary_operators.py:861`, `:883`), both in `numerics/`, and the production subclass `ReactionRateFunctional(InnerProductFunctional)` (`transport/reaction_rate_functional.py:76`, named in 23 production and 29 test files, the row of the production fission dyad `outer(chi, production_rate)`, `transport/operators/fission.py:131,:294`, `isotropic_transfer.py:439-479`) lives in `transport/`; moving the retraction body to `ReactionRateFunctional` would make that `isinstance` test name a `transport` class from `numerics`, an import edge the layer gate forbids (`tests/gates/test_layer_imports.py:95`, `FORBIDDEN_EDGES["numerics"]` holds the L2 packages) (`[M]` `check_structure_N09.md`, `n09_probe1.py`, `check_elegance_N09.md` finding 1). The 4 production and 7 test files that name `InnerProductFunctional` (an `ast` count of files with a `Name` or `Attribute` node of that name; a text grep finds 10 test files, `[M]` `git grep -c` in check 3 Q5, and the difference is prose or string mentions `[R]`) are therefore not re-pointed; W2 retires the class with the one-axis retraction family. The alternative, retiring it with `ReactionRateFunctional` owning the body, is SD-33's first option and is not taken for the reason above.

### 3.8 `numerics/trial_test.py` (new) and `transport/four_spaces.py` (new; concept `four`)

- `Testing(Enum)` (**`# concept: pair | role: component | kind: set`**, P2-13(b)): `NODE`, `INTEGRAL`; the enum sets `__test__ = False` so that pytest does not collect it (`[M]` `check2/test_n08_collect_probe.py`: importing a class whose name begins `Test` into a gate module prints `PytestCollectionWarning`).
- `TrialTest` (frozen, **`# concept: pair | role: head | kind: element`**, P2-13(b): the pair is mathematics and lives in `numerics/`, and the concept `pair` has layer `numerics`): fields `trial: FunctionSpace`, `test: FunctionSpace`, `testing: tuple[tuple[tuple[str, ...], str, Testing], ...]` (summand path, factor label, testing: the bulk summand and each trace summand are tested per summand, and two summands that share a factor label can be tested differently; a one-level space has the empty path; check 2 elegance N08 E6). Invariants: same labels at each level of the sum tree; one `testing` entry per factor label of each summand; `NODE` is allowed exactly where `riesz` is defined on the derived source slot (one door, no separate form-presence test: a `NODE` label on a `NoForm` axis is refused through `riesz`, "riesz is not defined: "); per label the kind of `trial` is opposite to the kind of `test` ("trial and test kinds must be opposite"); a missing entry is refused ("no testing entry for label"). Derived in `__post_init__` (eager: a pair that cannot derive its spaces cannot be constructed): `source_space = test.dual().riesz(on=NODE labels)`, `response_space = trial.dual().riesz(on=NODE labels)`. There is NO `forward_kind` field.
- `FourSpaces` (frozen, **`# concept: four | role: head | kind: element`**, `transport/four_spaces.py`): `pair: TrialTest`; `flux = pair.trial`, `source = pair.source_space`, `importance = pair.test`, `response = pair.response_space`; `importance_source() -> DualPairing` and `response_flux() -> DualPairing` (added at N09, when `DualPairing` exists). `FieldRole` (`transport/fields/_bases.py:147`, already `component`, and an enum of TWO roles, FLUX and SOURCE_SINK, not the four) keeps its declaration and `RolePair` (`:165`, the head of `four` today) is re-tagged `component` (the head of `four` moves to `FourSpaces`), which retires the pinned `kind_law head_arm_kind` entry for `RolePair`; they are W3's retirement targets. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2/p2_13_simulate.out` (the tip with the new files added in memory, the tool's own checks, population stated in the script): the default (concept `pair` of kind `element`, layer `numerics`, head `TrialTest`, `Testing` its component; `FourSpaces` head of `four` holding a pair; `RolePair` `component`) mints 2 `role_test component_no_core_reader` rows (`FieldRole`, `RolePair`), 0 placement rows, one stale `kind_law` row (`RolePair`, which leaves), and one cycle-component edit (`pair` joins the 39-concept component); variant b2p (`FourSpaces` names `FieldRole`) mints 1 role row; the first draft (`Testing`, `TrialTest` components of `four` in `numerics/`, variant b3, the positive control) reproduces the check's 2 placement and 3 role rows. U8 asks the user.

**The production hook (check 2 elegance N11 E1, structure N11 F4; `[R]` the names).** `SNProblem.four_spaces -> FourSpaces` on the hub `sn/problem.py` (concept `four` joins its weld list, 8.1): the `TrialTest` of the bulk (+) trace direct sum, with `trial` equal to the space the solver allocates the flux on (an identity, not a cell equality), the `test` declared factor by factor (bulk: the importance row of 7.13; each trace summand: its angular test factor is `V/tang` with `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)`, so the derivation of its source holds, G08c), and `testing` `{angular: NODE, energy: INTEGRAL, spatial: INTEGRAL}` per summand path. N11 declares the pair in all three sharp methods (P2b-6, SD-32): the SN pair above, the diffusion pair (`DiffusionMesh.four_spaces`, `[R]` the name: the bulk and the hemispheric trace, whose summand `hemispheric_trace` builds, 3.6) and the homogeneous pair (`HomogeneousProblem.four_spaces`, `[R]` the name: energy only; 7.13 holds no 0-D row, so N11's opening writes it), so that G11a has one row per method and D29's "production declares the pair" has no method outside it; the factor-by-factor test declarations of the two added pairs are measured at N11's opening (`opening_N11.md`). The moment-layout pair stays a gate of N08 (G08b(1)). N11 re-points the hand derivations of N09 and N10 (`e.dual()`, `face.dual().riesz(...)`) to `problem.four_spaces`, so that no `.dual().riesz(` hand derivation of a role space remains at the five pairing sites (G11a, an `ast` arm).

### 3.9 Which states each type makes unspellable (summary)

| state | made unspellable by |
|---|---|
| a measure paired with a measure, or two integrated factors | `DualPairing` construction (L9) |
| a Riesz map on a formless or singular slot | `Factor` constructibility and A0 (S6, P2-1) |
| a half-range selected twice | one `half_range` (L12) |
| a weight that disagrees with its generator (#710) | `Axis` has no `weights=` on generator arms |
| a dense inverse with a rank read from a cutoff in `metric.py` | `DenseMetric(matrix, kernel=...)` |
| a quotient with two spellings | `Subquotient` normal form |
| a bulk-plus-trace space with two direct-sum classes | `DirectSumSpace` is the only sum arm |
| a source space declared by hand | `TrialTest` derivation (L11) |
| the face area outside the metric | the face-spatial factor's form (L15, N10) |
| the orientation inside the metric; an orientation-blind leakage | `Orientation.sign()` as the partner function of the pairing (L15) |
| a pairing whose value moves with the representative of a subquotient | the subquotient clause of `DualPairing` (S12) |
| a factor minted with a placeholder variance | `Factor` has no default variance and no `Factor.on` (3.1) |
| a formed factor on a singular form with no declared quotient | `Factor` constructibility (P2-1, S6) |
| a summand label spelled twice | the label declared once by the builder (P2-5, 3.5) |
| a half-range view that reads a gauge coordinate | the builder's refusal "K meets a half-range" (L16) |

## 4. The two contracts of chart ruling B10, as exact attribute lists (D25, D26)

**Contract 1: what a factor reads from its generator (W8a builds behind it and may change everything else).** A factor's axis is minted by one of the origin-specific constructors of 3.2 (`of_measure`, `of_quadrature`, `of_basis`, or the bare constructor with the `FromFrame` or `Declared` origin); each of the five `Origin` arms exposes exactly the attributes of rows 2 to 6 and the axis reads nothing else, each once, at the mint; row 1, `index_shape`, is the constructor's argument and not an arm attribute (`[M]` `check_structure_N03.md` F4: no arm exposes it today) (D25 is consumers 2.1 minus its attributes 4 `variance` and 5 `testing`; today the arms expose `kind`, `support`, `nodes`, `layout` and `determined_weights`, the last replaced by `form`; `Declared` holds its form, `Declared(kind, form)`, and `FromQuadrature` holds the optional `face_axis`, so the face-weighted form is `FromQuadrature`'s answer and the origin stays `FromQuadrature`; no `FactorGenerator` Protocol is built, SD-21):

| # | attribute | type | read for |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `index_shape` (the constructor's argument) | `tuple[int, ...]`, rank >= 0 (P2-2) | the axis shape |
| 2 | `kind` | `AxisKind` = `NODAL \| Modal(unit)` | the coordinate kind |
| 3 | `form` | `Form` on `index_shape` (a `DiagonalMetric`, a `DenseMetric` with its declared kernel and signature, or `NoForm`) | the one metric home (today `Axis.weights`, the overlay Gram, `inner_product_weights`) |
| 4 | `support` | `Manifold \| None` | identity only |
| 5 | `nodes` | `NDArray \| None` | identity only |
| 6 | `layout` | `Layout \| None` | identity and the index structure the member contract reads |

The generator does not supply variance (the factor owns it) or testing (the pair owns it). Refuted for this question: variance declared by the generator (the same quadrature serves a node-tested and an integral-tested scheme) and testing on the factor (QA F6). What the generator DECLARES rather than leaves to the factor to detect: the kernel of its form (a `DiagonalMetric` reads it exactly from zero weights; a `DenseMetric` receives it as `kernel`). The generator handle is retained as `Axis.origin` (identity excludes it), so the seven consumers that narrow `axis.origin` by `match` (`transport/frames/harmonic_frame.py:441`, `:484`, `sn/mesh/reduced_operator.py:517`, `sn/angular/closure.py:1606`, `:2168`, `transport/fields/_bases.py:548`, `numerics/axis_marginal.py:446`, the last being the `case FromBasis(basis) if CellLayout` guard that SD-8's `NoForm` makes unnecessary; `[M]` `check_structure_N03.md` F14, which re-read the anchors: the first draft's `:1610` and `:2169` and its count of six were off) keep working; what they read through it (`level_indices`, `mu_x`, `ndim`, `factors[i].n`) is not part of this contract and is W8a's to type `[M]` m2 4.1.

**Contract 2: what the trace reads from a face (W6 builds behind it).** Five attributes of `FaceSpec` and one of the angular generator. From the face the trace reads `axis_index` and `sign` (with `key`, `cell_shape`, `measure`); from the angular generator it reads the cosine along an axis, through `Axis.of_quadrature(..., face_axis=axis_index)`, never as a caller-supplied array:

| # | attribute | type | read for |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `FaceSpec.key` | `str` | the summand label and identity (today the string `"xmin"`) |
| 2 | `FaceSpec.axis_index` | `int` | which direction cosine is the face normal's |
| 3 | `FaceSpec.sign` | `+1 \| -1` | the outward sign of the normal along that axis |
| 4 | `FaceSpec.cell_shape` | `tuple[int, ...]`, `()` on a 1-D mesh | the face's own spatial factor |
| 5 | `FaceSpec.measure` | `NDArray` of shape `cell_shape` (a 0-d array when `cell_shape == ()`: one slot, one behaviour) | the face-cell areas, the face-spatial form (R9, #680); the one source of the face-cell area (P2-11) |
| 6 | the angular generator's cosine along an axis: `Quadrature.mean_axis_cosine(axis_index)` (`directional.py:299`) | `NDArray (n_ordinates,)` | the cosines of L12, read inside `Axis.of_quadrature(..., face_axis=)` and by `Orientation` (outward cosine = `sign` times it) |

No declared tangent set (D26: the tangential set is the zero set of the cosines, exactly, L12). No face-name parsing: `geometry.faces.face_normal(name)` parses `"{axis}{min|max}"` today `[M]` m2 2.10 and stays until W6's typed face makes attributes 2 and 3 fields. Until W6 lands, one adapter `trace_faces(mesh)` in `transport/phase_spaces.py` reads the mesh's `FaceLabel`, the areas (1-D) and the transverse widths (d >= 2, where `MaterialMesh.areas` raises) and yields `FaceSpec`s, so that `FaceSpec.measure` is the one source of the face-cell area (P2-11); W6 deletes it (a declared edge `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[face-adapter] machinery: a typed Face (W6)`).

---

## 5. The fate of every existing class (`scratch/ontology/w1/measure/m2_asis_spaces.md` sections 1 and 2)

Population: the 36 production classes the concept table declares on W1's 12 concepts, the 8 classes declared elsewhere whose weld list names a W1 concept, and the classes the retired spaces drag with them (`FaceSlot`, the three radial characteristic classes). Verdicts: **kept** (unchanged), **re-spelled** (same name, new body or tag), **retired** (deleted; no shim, so every call site is re-pointed in the same unit), **renamed**. The unit column is the build unit of section 6.

| class (file) | concept, role today | verdict | reason | unit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Functional` (`numerics/functional.py:150`) | covector, interface | kept | the co-vector interface; `DualPairing` conforms | |
| `Covector` (`numerics/space.py:157`) | covector, head | re-spelled | `evaluate` stays the canonical contraction and `DualPairing.__call__` delegates to it (N09); minted by `flat` (the partial application of the pairing, D17, the same object as `DualPairing.covector` on the form legs) and by the carrier hook (N04); SD-42(b) | N04 and N09 |
| `CoupledSpace` (`numerics/coupled_system.py:443`) | dsum, head | retired | the N-ary direct sum; becomes `DirectSumSpace` | N05 |
| `FullFieldSpace` (`numerics/spaces/full_field_space.py:165`) | dsum, twin | retired | the 2-ary direct sum, `bulk (+) trace`; chart ruling B12 assigns all 15 production code files (section 6, N05) | N05 |
| `DualSpace` (`numerics/space.py:1514`) | dual, head | retired | duality is a factor property; `dual.axis(label)` raises today (`TypeError`); alive from N04 to N06 only as the dual of the legacy leaves (6.5) | N07 |
| `Axis` (`numerics/axis.py:461`) | factor, head, weld `factor, fmetric` | re-spelled | gains `form` and `restriction`; loses `weights=` and the `weights` field; role becomes weld (`welded: factor, fmetric`) because the head moves to `Factor`, and its debt entry stays until W8a (P2b-3) | N03 |
| `EnergyAxis` (`:659`) | factor, arm | re-spelled | `form = NoForm`; keeps its present role (`arm`), P2b-3 | N03 |
| `BasisKind`, `Modal`, `Declared`, `FromBasis`, `FromMeasure`, `FromQuadrature` | factor, component/part | kept (`FromMeasure`, `FromQuadrature` lose their `weights=` path) | unit-bearing coordinate kind is orthogonal to variance; origin is provenance | N03 |
| `FromFrame` | factor, component | re-spelled | answers `form` as the frame's Gram `DenseMetric` with the frame's declared kernel | N03 |
| `DenseMetric` (`numerics/metric.py:512`) | fmetric, arm | re-spelled | `kernel` argument, exact-count `svd` inverse, no `rcond`, no supplied-inverse path | N01 |
| `DiagonalMetric` (`:417`) | fmetric, arm | re-spelled | `counting(n)`, negative refusal, exact kernel and signature | N03 |
| `FactoredMetric` (`:696`) | fmetric, arm | re-spelled | slot-order Kronecker over blocks; entries typed `Form` (energy `NoForm`, a formed axis never `None`) | N04 |
| `DualMetric` (`:836`), `HilbertMetric` (`:305`) | fmetric, arm / head | kept | | |
| `PositionedMetric` (`:289`) | fmetric, interface | retired if no reader remains after the overlay goes, else kept `[R]` (the opening of N04 runs the AST) | positioned entries were the overlay's mechanism | N04 |
| `FieldRole` (`transport/fields/_bases.py:147`), `RolePair` (`:165`) | four, component / head | re-spelled (role tag only) | the head of `four` moves to `FourSpaces`; both are W3's retirement targets; the `kind_law head_arm_kind` debt entry for `RolePair` retires | N08 |
| `HarmonicLayout`, `LegendreLayout`, `CellLayout`, `ModeLayout`, `PolynomialLayout`, `TensorLayout` (`numerics/coefficient_layout.py`) | layout, arm | kept | | |
| `FaceLayout` (`numerics/face_layout.py:146`) | layout, arm | renamed `CoproductLayout` | the ordered coproduct of named blocks with offsets: exactly what `DirectSumSpace` needs; 4 offset loops become one | N05 |
| `FaceSlot` (`:89`) | layout, part | renamed `LayoutBlock` | N05 |
| `InnerProductFunctional` (`numerics/functional.py:186`) | pairing, head, weld `pairing, natural, multiplier` | kept until W2, re-declared | the head role moves to `DualPairing` at N09 and the class becomes `# concept: pairing \| role: weld \| kind: element.covector \| welded: pairing, natural, multiplier`; its body, a one-axis retraction by a weight (W2's family), stays under `ELEGANCE-DEBT[retraction] W2`, because `RankOneOperator` in `numerics/` decides the fission transpose by `isinstance(self.functional, InnerProductFunctional)` and its production subclass `ReactionRateFunctional` lives in `transport/` (3.7, SD-33, P2b-8); its two debt entries (`weld`, `covector_off_lineage`) stay | N09 |
| `AngularFaceTraceSpace` (`numerics/spaces/angular_trace_space.py:775`) | subspace, head | retired | a subspace carried as data with no parent link and a name-based identity (#684); its outflow and inflow tiers become restricted axes and its full tier the `V/K` factor (6.7) | N06 |
| `FunctionSpace` (`numerics/space.py:195`) | tensor, weld `tensor, fmetric, factor` | re-spelled | the abstract type with an allow-list of arms from N04 and exactly two arms from N07; the product arm leaves it at N04 and the named-leaf arm at N07 (SD-18, SD-23) | N04 and N07 |
| `TensorProductSpace` (`:1389`) | tensor, head | retired | one tensor product, `TensorSpace` | N04 |
| `AngularTraceSpace` (`angular_trace_space.py:328`) | trace, weld `trace, dsum, fmetric` | retired | a direct sum over faces with a flat weight slot and no axes; becomes `trace_space(...)` | N06 |
| `ScalarTraceSpace` (`scalar_trace_space.py:59`) | trace, weld | retired | becomes `hemispheric_trace(...)` | N06 |
| `_RadialCharacteristicSubSpace` (`radial_characteristic_space.py:324`), `RadialCharacteristicInteriorSpace`, `RadialCharacteristicBoundarySpace` (`:485`) | rayspace, weld `rayspace, trace` | retired | they subclass `FunctionSpace`, which becomes closed on two arms; re-spelled as the builder functions `radial_interior_space` and `radial_boundary_space` returning axis-built `DirectSumSpace`s with unchanged values (section 3.6, SD-11) | N06 |
| `TraceRestrictionOperator` (`numerics/elementary_operators.py:300`) | an operator | re-spelled | rebuilt on the new outflow/inflow tensor spaces; its laws `R E = id` and `R^dagger = E` are re-gated; W2 generalises it | N06 |
| `AxisRetractionOperator` (`numerics/axis_marginal.py:147`) | natural | re-spelled | `_mint` reads the factor's axis (a restricted axis for a half-range view); its `TypeError` at `space.py:583` goes with the axes-less leaves; its zero-mass cutoff `64 eps scale` (`:485`) is W2's (it reads the declared kernel then); no W1 gate reads it | N04 |
| `SystemRestrictionOperator` (`numerics/coupled_system.py:1397`) | | re-spelled | re-pointed to `DirectSumSpace.summand(label)`; a summand projection reached by label; W2 builds the keyed block operator | N05 |
| `Member` (`numerics/space.py:120`) | member | kept | W3 | |
| `CompositeField`, `CoupledField`, `Composite` | member | kept | W3 retires them; 36 test files pin `CoupledField` (chart ruling B11) | |
| `CellCoefficient` (`data/cells.py:95`, concept parameter, welds covector) | | kept | W3 | |
| `DiscreteMeasure` (`numerics/measure.py:224`, concept measure, welds factor) | | kept | W8a | |
| `OctantSweep` (`sn/loss_representation/sweep_schedule.py:99`, schedule, welds subspace), `LegendreMomentTransfer` (`transport/operators/transfer.py:131`, transfer, welds subspace) | | kept | W4 re-spells their gathers on the `Subquotient` | |
| `SNProblem` (`sn/problem.py:100`, hub, welds trace, dsum), `BulkField` (`_bases.py:338`), `MomentField` (`:779`) | | kept (SNProblem and the field modules are re-pointed to the successors of the retired spaces) | W5 / W3 | N04, N05, N06 and N11 (the hub gains `four_spaces`, 8.1, P2b-6) |

Concepts: after this wave the head of `factor` is `Factor`, of `dual` is `Variance`, of `tensor` is `TensorSpace` and of `dsum` is `DirectSumSpace` until N07, when both concepts merge into a new concept `space` whose head is the named closed sum `SpaceArms` of the two arms and whose `component` is `FunctionSpace` (the default of P2-13(a), U8), of `pair` is `TrialTest` (a new concept, `Testing` its component, P2-13(b)), of `subspace` is `Subquotient`, of `pairing` is `DualPairing`, of `four` is `FourSpaces`, of `trace` is the module `numerics/trace.py`, `layout` stays headless with `CoproductLayout` replacing `FaceLayout` among its arms, and `quotient` leaves the table (U5).

---

## 6. The units

### 6.0 Conventions of this section

- **The order is leaves first** (amendment decision A1). A unit that builds a type on another unit's type comes after it, so no production factor is ever minted with a placeholder variance and no type is built twice. The chain is `N00` (harness), then `N01` and `N02` (the only pair built at the same time), then `N03` (factor), `N04` (tensor-core), `N05` (direct-sum), `N06` (trace), `N07` (closing), `N08` (four), `N09` (pairing), `N10` (trace-area), `N11` (production-typing). The table old unit to new unit is in the amendment record at the end.
- **A unit's file set is its call-site set** (chart ruling B7): production, tests, `tools/`, `derivations/`. `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend1/incidence_amend1.py` (AST over 263 `orpheus` + 144 `orpheus/derivations` + 974 `tests` + 59 `tools` files, from the raw census of `measure_callsites.md`, re-run on the new unit boundaries; output `incidence_amend1.json`, `.out`; positive control: `FullFieldSpace` reads 15 production files, equal to `measure_callsites.md`): `derivations/` and `tools/` name 0 of the W1 symbols for 32 of 33 rows (`measure_callsites.md` section 1; the 33rd, `.norm(`, is a numpy call), so **no unit re-points `derivations/` or `tools/`** (chart ruling A2 closes for W1). The production lists below are the files whose AST names the unit's symbol set; the test counts are the same predicate over `tests/`; a builder recomputes its own test set with the predicate script of 6.13 (a worktree has no `scratch/`). A test file that names a symbol only in prose (docstring, comment, message) is edited for the prose only: `[M]` 23 `orpheus` and 32 `tests` files carry a retiring name only in prose (`measure_callsites.md` section 2).
- **Serial order and shared files.** A serial unit may re-edit a file an earlier unit merged (D30); units built at the same time share no file. `[M]` N01 and N02 share 0 production and 0 test files (`incidence_amend1.json`, pair `N01 dense-inverse | N02 half-range`: `{orpheus: 0, tests: 0}`; P2b-5 adds `sn/sweep/cache.py` and `numerics/quadrature/directional.py` to N02, and neither is among N01's four production files `[M]` by the lists of 6.2 and 6.3; the test files that name the two added constants are counted at N02's opening `[R]`); N02 edits none of `docs/architecture/concept_table.toml`, `concept_debt.toml`, `concept_table.rst`, `numerics/__init__.py` or `.github/workflows/gates.yml`; N01 edits `numerics/__init__.py` (an export) and `concept_table.rst` (its three declaration classes carry `concept: subspace`, so the generated page changes), which N02 does not touch, so the pair still shares no file (check 2 structure N00-N02 F9). N08 and N09 are NOT built at the same time: both edit `numerics/__init__.py` `[R]`. The three space-hub units share production files: N04 and N05 share 19, N04 and N06 share 14, N05 and N06 share 13 (`[M]` `incidence_amend1.json`), so they stay serial units, each green on its own (SD-5).
- **Green on its own.** At each unit's last commit the unit's gates (section 7), the harness (N00) and the 5 Step A files (W1-P6) pass, the layer gate and the three concept-table gates (W1-P7) pass, and no test outside the unit's file set changes its result. Run per file, one process each (`[M]` m1: one process over all 24 xfail files stalled; each file alone runs).
- **Bit identity.** "Identical" means `.tobytes()` equal, never `allclose`. The instrument is the N00 harness plus the unit's own spy. Every unit except N10 leaves every harness snapshot byte-equal. **Identity digests and the derived space names that embed them move unit by unit, and the unit plan table 6.0a states per unit which and why (P2-6).** N01: every space that holds a dense form, because the declared kernel is a compare field of `DenseMetric` (`[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n01_identity_probe.py`: one added compare field moves the digest; the count of such spaces is owed at N01's opening). N03: every axis-built space, because the axis key's `weights` part is replaced by `form` and `restriction` joins it (`FunctionSpace.identity_key` embeds `self.axes`, so the digest of an axis-built space follows its axes' keys; check 2 structure N03 F5; P2b-1, SD-28: no transitional encoder is kept, and 0 literal pins of a space digest exist `[M]` `measure_callsites.md` 4.1). N04: every axis-built space again, because the variance joins every factor's identity key. N05 and N06: the production full-field, coupled, trace and radial spaces, because their classes are replaced. N10: the SN trace summands (`Diag(A_f)` is form content). No other unit moves a digest.
- **No second product type at any commit, and no placeholder variance at any commit** (amendment A1). N04 deletes the concrete product (`TensorProductSpace` and the axis-built arm of `FunctionSpace`) in the commit that adds `TensorSpace`; from N04 every production builder states the variance of each factor literally, as its layout declares it (decision D16): the flux space is typed `MEASURE` on every slot, density on the formed axes and integrated on energy, as the table of 7.13 states (as of its draft: 7.13 is NOT FINAL and N08's opening re-derives it). There is no window in which the flux is typed as a function.
- **The store.** `[M]` `measure_callsites.md` 4.2: no memo key holds a space (0 of 27 reachable dataclass types is a `FunctionSpace` or `Axis`), so identity changes stale nothing through the key; but the memo is source-driven: 2117 of 2122 live entries pin 14 files of W1's sets (`data/materials.py`, `numerics/{axis, __init__, assembled_operator, coupled_system, elementary_operators, field, frame_faces, functional, metric, operator, space, tangential}.py`, `numerics/quadrature/ordinate_pairing.py`) and an edit to any of them stales the same 2117, not additively; deleting `numerics/spaces/*.py` modules stales 0. The first merge that changes a pinned def is N01 (`metric.py`, `frame_faces.py`). Rule (SD-9, section 11; the user is informed in section 10, item U12): the orchestrator takes the W0 snapshot (`scratch/ontology/w0/memo/w10_retake_snapshot.sh`) before N01 and runs the witness comparison (`w7_witness_compare.py`, stored VALUES bit for bit) once after N11, since each later merge re-stales the regenerated entries. **N00 measures the unexplained growth of the live set by 101 entries since W0's 2021 (`measure_callsites.md` 4.2: 2122 live now) before anything is edited**: the harness unit counts the live entries on the clean tip with the instrument of `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/probe/memo_presence.py` (it calls the real `Store.lookup`) and records the count and its difference from 2021 in `MANIFEST.txt`.
- **Concept table and debt.** Each unit that retires a class removes its debt entry and edits the table rows named in section 8 in the same commit, and regenerates `docs/architecture/concept_table.rst` with `.venv/bin/python -m tools.concept_table.page` (`--check` verifies it; `python -m tools.concept_table` alone prints usage and exits 2, `[M]` rerun for pass 2 and `check_structure_N07.md` F3; its subcommands are `report` and `seed`), gate `test_concept_table_page.py`. A unit that edits the body of a weld-listed class also runs `python -m tools.concept_table seed`, or `test_seed_fingerprints_reproduce_on_the_tree` reds, and where a class leaves the weld list the role change and the body deletion land in ONE commit.
- **Gate files** live in `tests/gates/numerics/w1/` (a new package with `__init__.py`), the gates that build a `FourSpaces` in `tests/gates/transport/w1/` (SD-15, W1-P5), except where a re-pointed existing gate is named. Each unit's gate files are added in the same commit to the one CI step "The space algebra gates (W1)" of `.github/workflows/gates.yml` (created in N03, which lists the gate files of N00, N01 and N02 as well, because `gates.yml` runs only explicit lists, so otherwise twelve gates never reach CI: check 2 structure N00-N02 F8).
- **Names.** The done-when rows are `W1-P1` to `W1-P10`; the hand pairings of `measure_pairings.md` are `HP1` to `HP10` (HP1 `numerics/posing.py:55`, HP2 `numerics/iteration.py:1590`, HP3 `sn/solver.py:1592`, HP4 `sn/solver.py:1649`, HP5 `sn/solver.py:1756`, HP6 `sn/solution.py:485`, HP7 `sn/solution.py:651`, HP8 `sn/solution.py:690`, HP9 `sn/solution.py:826`, HP10 `homogeneous/solver.py:149`); the section's objects are `S1` to `S13`; this specification's own decisions are `SD-1` to `SD-43` (`SD-6` and `SD-7` of the first draft were deleted); the synthesis's decisions are `D1` to `D32` and the chart's rulings keep their `O`, `A`, `B`, `R`, `U` ids.

### 6.0a The unit plan (pass 2)

The order is amendment pass 1's and stands (leaves first). For each unit: its place, its outcome, what it depends on, what it deletes, the identity digests it moves with the reason (P2-6), and the harness snapshot it re-points (P2-15). Each unit's own section (6.x, 7.x) is NOT FINAL until the unit opens (below).

| N01 `dense-inverse` | 2, parallel with N02 | `numerics/declaration.py` (rostered), `DenseMetric(matrix, *, kernel)` with the exact-count inverse in one body for both faces, the frame's declared kernel and `FRAME_GRAM_RANK_RTOL` | N00 | the Penrose admission, the public `inverse_matrix=`, `_DENSE_METRIC_RCOND` | every space that holds a dense form: the declared kernel is a compare field | none |

The design layer cites some numbers and tables of NOT FINAL sections (the 12 members of 6.5, the literal arguments of 6.10 and the numbers of 6.11, the table of 7.13); each such sentence says in place that it is as of the draft of that section and is re-measured at the unit's opening.

Until a unit opens, its sections 6.x and 7.x carry the first line "NOT FINAL: finalised at this unit's opening. Open findings: `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_<unit>.md`." and nothing in them that a design ruling of pass 2 contradicts, apart from the edits listed in the amendment record; the opening file lists the unit's findings from the per-unit check, the rulings of pass 2 the unit implements and the measurements it owes. A unit is finalised on the tree the previous unit left: its opening measurements, the amendment on its findings, a per-unit check of the final text, then the build.

### 6.1 N00 `harness`: the bit-identity instrument, rebuilt first (D28)

NOT FINAL: finalised at this unit's opening. Open findings: `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_N00.md`.

- **Outcome.** Typed-probe snapshots, stored in the tracked tree, of: (a) the dense pencil `A`, the fission production `F` and the boundary coupling `B` of three fixtures (an SN slab, a diffusion slab, an SN sphere); (b) the flat trace-space weights of the SN fixtures and the diffusion slab (the bytes of `metric_diagonal()` of every trace-side space); (c) the `matrix` and `inverse_matrix` of every dense-metric frame the fixtures build; (d) the `apply_metric` and `apply_inverse_metric` of each fixture's bulk, full-field and radial spaces on one fixed random vector each (seeded, `np.random.default_rng(20261123)`), all compared with `.tobytes()`; (e) the live-entry count of the reference store on the clean tip (C4: the 101-entry growth since W0 is explained or reported before any edit). A 1-ulp perturbation control (`np.nextafter`) of one entry of each snapshot must read different.
- **Why first.** `[M]` design_tree section 1: the prototype's 77 gates do not import on the tip (0 of 77 run), and `[M]` `measure_pairings.md` section 0: a one-ulp perturbation reads different and the reverse slot order reads different on 15,625 of 32,318 bulk Riesz applications (so the instrument discriminates).
- **Files.** Creates `tests/gates/numerics/w1/__init__.py`, `_snapshot.py` (the capture and compare helper), `test_snapshot_harness.py`, `snapshots/*.npz` and `snapshots/MANIFEST.txt` (one line per array: fixture, name, shape, dtype, sha1 of the bytes). Edits: none in `orpheus/`, none in existing tests. Deletes nothing. Depends on: nothing. The fixtures are built through the public constructors the existing gates use (an SN slab as in `tests/gates/sn/eigenvalue/test_keff_slab.py`, a diffusion slab as in `tests/gates/diffusion/test_solver.py`, an SN sphere as in `tests/gates/sn/operators/test_dual_metric_sn.py`: the builder reads those three and records in `MANIFEST.txt` which builder each fixture reuses; `[R]` these three files are the ones the spy population of `measure_pairings.md` used and were not re-read for this draft).
- **Green on its own:** the harness gates (7.1) pass on the tip by construction.
- **Re-baselines:** none. **Bit identity:** the snapshot is taken at the tip. The first unit to move a snapshot is N10 (trace weights), which re-takes (b) with the reason in its commit.

### 6.7 N06 `trace`: one builder; the trace is a direct sum over faces, typed once (D20, D21, D23; amendments A2, A3, A7, A8)

NOT FINAL: finalised at this unit's opening. Open findings: `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_N06.md`.

- **Outcome.** `numerics/trace.py` (`FaceSpec`, `Orientation`, `trace_space`, `hemispheric_trace`); `numerics/spaces/radial_characteristic_space.py` keeps the walk helpers and gains the two builder functions of amendment A7 (3.6); `AngularTraceSpace`, `AngularFaceTraceSpace`, `ScalarTraceSpace` and the three radial characteristic classes are deleted; each trace summand is a `TensorSpace` `<angular, energy, spatial>` (and the face-moment factor where the scheme has a face basis, P2-3) built once, with the angular factor `V/tang` on an axis whose form is `Diag(|cos| w)` and whose kernel is read exactly from the zero weights; `outflow(face)` and `inflow(face)` are tensor spaces whose angular axis is `Axis.restricted(indices)` (amendment A2); `TraceRestrictionOperator` is rebuilt on them; `current(face)` is `summand.without_axis("angular")`, the summand's own energy and spatial factors with their variances unchanged. There is no second typing step: the trace is minted with `sk = V/tang` once.
- **Which of today's half-tier spaces become what** (read from `AngularTraceSpace._face_spaces`, `angular_trace_space.py:598-646`, and `_current_spaces` `:669-706`): the `outflow` and `inflow` tiers (`AngularFaceTraceSpace` with `role` outflow or inflow, shape `(|indices|, *slot.shape[1:])`, weights `face_metric[indices]`, `ordinate_indices`, `directions`) become **restricted axes**: their arrays keep their shape (`(4, 2)` on `Quadrature.product(4, 4)`), the angular factor's axis is `Axis.restricted(indices)` with whole `sk` and its parent recorded; the `full` tier becomes **the `V/tang` factor on the full axis** (shape `(n, ...)` as today); `current_space` (`FunctionSpace(name, shape=slot.shape[1:], inner_product_weights=ones)`) has no angular slot and becomes `summand.without_axis("angular")` (3.6), not a name-built mint. 2 of the 4 tiers are restricted axes, 1 is a quotient factor, 1 has no angular factor. `AxisRetractionOperator.of(outflow, "angular")` resolves because the restricted axis is the factor's axis (W1-P1).

### 6.13 The predicate script (for a builder with no `scratch/`)

A unit's test call-site set is computed by this script; it parses with `ast` and counts a file when it contains the name as an `ast.Name`, an `ast.Attribute` attribute, an import alias, a keyword argument or a `def`/`class` name (a name only in a docstring, comment or string is excluded: prose-only files are edited for prose). Positive control `[M]`: run on `FullFieldSpace` at `b5f18d4e` it reads 15 production files and 20 test files, and on `DenseMetric` 4 and 21, equal to `measure_callsites.md` section 1 and to N01's set. It does not read string annotations (so a file naming a class only in an annotation string is not counted; `measure_callsites.md` counted those).

```python
import ast, pathlib, sys
def names(tree):
    out = set()
    for n in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(n, ast.Name): out.add(n.id)
        elif isinstance(n, ast.Attribute): out.add(n.attr)
        elif isinstance(n, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)): out |= {a.name.split(".")[-1] for a in n.names}
        elif isinstance(n, ast.keyword) and n.arg: out.add(n.arg)
        elif isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)): out.add(n.name)
    return out
def files(root, symbols):
    hits = []
    for p in pathlib.Path(root).rglob("*.py"):
        if "derivations" in p.parts and root == "orpheus": continue
        try: tree = ast.parse(p.read_text())
        except SyntaxError: continue
        if names(tree) & set(symbols): hits.append(str(p))
    return sorted(hits)
```

---

## 7. The gates of each unit

Format. Each gate has an id, its input, the state in today's tree that reddens it (**first red**), and the in-process mutation that reddens it once the unit is green. Every row carries a mutation that can redden (amendment decision B2); a gate that asserts an `ast` predicate has as its mutation "run the same predicate on the module source with the forbidden definition appended to a copy, and require red", which is the positive control of the instrument (instrument doctrine X1). Mutations are applied by `monkeypatch` in-process and restored by pytest (never `git checkout`, `git restore` or `git stash`; a crash-safe restore is a `monkeypatch` fixture, not a `try/finally`). "Import-only red" means the only red today is that the module or name does not exist; the behavioural first red is then the first one the gate can show, named beside it. A gate that greps a message greps the quoted fragment of section 3, never the class name. Every gate's file is under `tests/gates/numerics/w1/` unless a path is given. The gates are specified in this text; a gate "written from the plan cannot catch an error in the plan" (chart, step 1), so each lists its own red and its own mutation. A row whose first cell begins `::` lives in the file of the nearest row above it that names a file. Gate ids follow the new unit numbers (`G<unit><letter>`); the old-to-new table is in the amendment record. Every law of 2.2 has a gate: L1 G04a, L2 G04k, L3 G03a, L4 G04k, L5 G04k and G11d, L6 G05c, L7 G03l, L8 G04e, L9 G03c and G09a, L10 G03b, G04l and G09f, L11 G08a, L12 G02a and G02b, L13 G05d and G05e, L14 G08e, L15 G10a and G10b; L16 and L17, added by pass 2, have no gate yet (owed: `opening_N06.md`, `opening_N11.md`).

### 7.13 R14's table as the gate computes it

NOT FINAL: finalised at N08's opening (G08a computes this table). Open findings: `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/opening_N08.md`. The design layer (3.4a, 3.8, W1-P10, 6.0) cites this table as of its draft.

| role | space | angular | energy | spatial |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| flux | trial | `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` | `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)` | `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` |
| source | `test.dual().riesz(on={angular})` | `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` | `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)` | `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)` |
| importance | test | `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` | `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` | `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` |
| response | `trial.dual().riesz(on={angular})` | `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` | `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` | `(FUNCTION, INTEGRATED)` |

`[R]` derived by hand from L11 in this draft (kind and chart flip under `dual`; `riesz` on the node-tested angular slot flips the chart back), and equal to `measure_pairings.md` section 1's role table (flux `A(+,M) E(-,M) X(+,M)`, source `A(+,M) E(-,M) X(-,M)`, importance `A(+,F) E(+,F) X(+,F)`, response `A(+,F) E(+,F) X(-,F)`; `+` density, `-` integrated), which `[M]` probe `p1_variance` reproduced for 12 of 12 cells. Pairings: importance with source: angular both `DENSITY` (the form leg), energy opposite kinds (canonical), spatial opposite (canonical); response with flux likewise. `(flux, source)` is refused on energy (same kind, both integrated). From N04 the flux row is what the production builders mint; N08 derives the other three rows from the pair and N11 makes production declare the pair.

---

## 8. The concept-table and debt-file edits

All edits are made by the unit that retires the class (section 6) and by no other; each unit regenerates `docs/architecture/concept_table.rst` with `.venv/bin/python -m tools.concept_table.page` (`python -m tools.concept_table` alone prints usage and exits 2; 6.0) and runs the gates `test_concept_table.py`, `test_concept_table_machinery.py` and `test_concept_table_page.py`. The table is `docs/architecture/concept_table.toml` (88 concepts); the debt file is `docs/architecture/concept_debt.toml` (lists `twin`, `weld`, `role_test`, `misplaced`, `edge`, `cycle`, `kind_law`, `operator_off_algebra`, `covector_off_lineage`; the fingerprints are in `concept_seed.toml` and an entry leaves the debt file when its class leaves the list).

### 8.1 Concept rows (`concept_table.toml`)

| concept | edit | unit | reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dual` | `kind` space to set; `definition` "the variance of a factor: the pair (kind, chart) with the dual and Riesz involutions"; head `Variance` (moved from concept `factor`, where `Kind`, `VarianceChart` and `Variance` are components from N03 to N06 (SD-29), in the same commit that deletes `DualSpace`) | N07 | the head is a 4-element set with the Klein action (U8); the head and kind move only when the old head dies, so no commit has two heads |
| `factor` | `definition` "a factor of a space: an axis with its variance and its subquotient"; head `Factor` (was `Axis`) | N03 | D1 |
| `subspace` | `definition` "the subquotient S/K of a factor's coordinate space"; head `Subquotient` (was `AngularFaceTraceSpace`, deleted in the same commit); `theory` keeps `ref:bc-half-trace` | N06 | U5 |
| `quotient` | row removed (it was `missing`, no arms, `functions = ["orpheus.numerics.space.FunctionSpace.rank"]`); the constant `EXPECTED_CONCEPTS = 88` at `tests/gates/test_concept_table.py:36` becomes 87 in the same commit (`[M]` read: that file pins the concept count, at line 36 `[M]` `check_structure_N03.md` F10; then 86 at N07, when `tensor` and `dsum` merge into `space`, and 87 at N08, when `pair` is added) | N03 | U5: merged into `subspace`; `FunctionSpace.rank` stays an abstract method of the interface and carries no row |
| `tensor` | head `TensorSpace` (was `TensorProductSpace`); `functions` list re-pointed to `TensorSpace.identity_key`, `of_measure`, `of_basis`; at N07 the row merges into `space` (below) | N04 and N07 | D12, P2-13(a) |
| `dsum` | head `DirectSumSpace` (was `CoupledSpace`); at N07 the row merges into `space` (below) | N05 and N07 | D13, P2-13(a) |

The literal declaration comments of every new class are in section 3. The 12 classes of W1-P3's list leave the declarations with their files: 10 are deleted, and `FaceLayout` and `FaceSlot` are renamed `CoproductLayout` and `LayoutBlock` (section 5), so their old names have no `ClassDef` left; `PositionedMetric` is outside the list and is retired at N04 only if the opening's AST finds no reader (section 5). That is 5 of the 9 W1 debt entries leaving (plus the one W4 entry above); of the other four, three stay (the `weld` entry of `numerics/axis.py::Axis` until W8a types the generators, P2b-3, and the `weld` and `covector_off_lineage` entries of `numerics/functional.py::InnerProductFunctional` until W2, P2b-8) and the 9th, the cycle, shrinks but is not removed by W1 alone (`[M]` m2 section 1.3: removing every judged edge leaving a W1 concept leaves a 28-concept component and a W1-only 2-cycle `{dual, tensor}`; the 2-cycle goes with `DualSpace` in N07). An entry W1 adds to the debt file is the user's to rule: the only ones this text drafts are U8(b)'s 2 `role_test` rows, drafted on U8's default and put to the user as a question (the `ELEGANCE-DEBT[pairing-chart]` tags are code tags, not debt-file rows). Measured on the two defaults of U8 (`[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend2/p2_13_simulate.out`): the default of (a) adds none (the `FunctionSpace` weld entry leaves, the cycle entry gains `space` in place of `tensor`); the default of (b) adds 2 `role_test component_no_core_reader` rows (`FieldRole`, `RolePair`, both W3's retirement targets) and removes the `RolePair` `kind_law` row, and `pair` joins the cycle entry: (b) is drafted on its default, a new concept `pair` with 2 role rows until W3, and is a question to the user (U8(b), in the same words), and the variant that adds none is `FourSpaces` naming `FieldRole` and `RolePair` in accessors (0 rows, variant b2, which the ruling does not ask for). `Kind`, `VarianceChart` and `Variance` as components of `dual` from N03 would add 3 `component_no_core_reader` rows until N07 (`[M]` variant N03..N06), and SD-29 declares them components of `factor` to add none; keeping `Axis` a weld (3.2) adds none (`[M]` `amend2b/p2b_3_simulate.out`: 0 new, 0 stale), where re-tagging it a component would add 7 (SD-41, P2b-3).

|---|---|---|
