| W1 concepts in the 39-cycle | 9 `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend3/p3_1_cycle.py` (output `p3_1_cycle.out`: the 39-concept component of the tip holds all 9 W1 concepts, covector, dsum, dual, factor, four, pairing, subspace, tensor, trace) | at least 3 `[R]`, named by the text's own simulations: `pairing` (`InnerProductFunctional` stays a weld of it until W2, P2b-8; `[M]` `amend2b/p2b_8_simulate.out` variant v2: 0 new and 0 stale rows, and the variant that files the class under `natural` takes `pairing` out of the component and is not taken), `space` (it replaces `tensor` in the cycle entry at N07: `[M]` `amend2/p2_13_simulate.out` variants a3 and a3b, "cycle component gained: ['space'] lost: ['tensor']") and `pair` (it joins at N08: `[M]` the same file, variant b1, "gained: ['pair'] lost: []"). ## 11. Decisions for the orchestrator

Each is a choice the synthesis's decisions (D1 to D32) and the amendment decisions do not settle, with its structural reason. Sections 6 and 7 are drafted on it. `SD-6` and `SD-7` of the first draft were deleted by amendment decisions A1 and A2, and their numbers are not reused. **Contradictions between a decision and a measurement** are marked `CONTRADICTION` and sit first; the text applies the decision and marks the contradiction beside it.

**Contradictions and rulings of this pass.** Pass 3 records no new `CONTRADICTION`: the earlier ones keep their marks in their own entries (SD-17, SD-18, SD-19, SD-31, SD-33, SD-34, SD-37), and the rulings `P3-1` to `P3-7` are in section 13.

**Rulings of pass 2b on the open decisions of pass 2** (SD-28 to SD-42; each says what was chosen, the reason, and the alternative not taken with its structural reason; `CONTRADICTION` marks a ruling the code contradicts, applied as written with the contradiction beside it; SD-31, SD-34, SD-37, SD-39 and SD-40 record rulings applied in pass 2 and stand).

Alternative: restrict the frame head to a rank-1 flattened axis, which changes `Axis.shape` of today's heads and every slot shape that reads it.

- **SD-18 CONTRADICTION (amendment A1 against the code), ruled: five legacy leaf classes cannot sit under an abstract `FunctionSpace` that stores nothing without a copy of the named-leaf body.** `[M]` `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend1/legacy_inherit2.out`: `FunctionSpace` has 41 author-defined members; `AngularTraceSpace`, `ScalarTraceSpace`, `RadialCharacteristicInteriorSpace`, `RadialCharacteristicBoundarySpace` override 1 (`__repr__`), `AngularFaceTraceSpace` 2, and they inherit 40 (39) of 41, of which 28 (27) reach the stored fields `inner_product_weights`, `metric`, `axes` directly or through `self.<member>` calls. `[M]` `legacy_arms.out`: of the 13 members that read a stored field directly, 8 read `axes` only (the product arm, which N04 deletes), 0 read only the leaf fields, and 5 read both (`__post_init__` `space.py:346`, `identity_key` `:396`, `_overlay_forms` `:614`, `_axis_block` `:628`, `_resolved_metric` `:1016`): so a count of direct field reads gives 3 leaf members, and that is an undercount: `[M]` `legacy_axes_none.out` (the `axes is [not] None` tests) finds 12 members with a branch an axes-less leaf can execute (`__post_init__`, `identity_key`, `has_coordinate_cone`, `_axis_index`, `_overlay_forms`, `_axis_block`, `_with_overlay`, `without_axis`, `with_axis`, `positions_spanned_by`, `unit`, `_resolved_metric`), of which the leaves are READ as reaching 6 (`[R]`, settled by a spy at N04's opening). Taken (orchestrator ruling, pass 1b: default stands): the base keeps those branches and the four fields in place, tagged `ELEGANCE-DEBT[legacy-leaf] N07`, and N07 deletes them with `DualSpace`; no intermediate class, no copy (6.5). A per-class copy of the body (5 copies) was not taken, and moving N06 before N04 was not taken because it returns the placeholder-variance window. The tagged members are pinned by gate G04o (6.5, 7.5; pass 2 re-aims the pin at the set of member names carrying the tag and makes each body its leaf arm only, SD-38). The member set is as of the draft of 6.5 (NOT FINAL) and is re-measured at N04's opening.
- **SD-17 CONTRADICTION (amendment A5 against the code): `Axis.weights` has 2 production readers, not 8.** `[M]` `weights_readers.out` (AST, all `.weights` attribute reads in `orpheus/` outside `derivations/`: 87 reads in 23 files, receivers printed): the axis receivers are `numerics/space.py:1193` and `:1276`; the others are measures, quadratures, `DiagonalMetric`, `ResponseNetwork`. `[M]` `axisw_spy.json` (runtime, 275 gate tests): outside `axis.py` the reads are `space.py:1193` (259,990), `space.py:1276` (1) and the generic content encoder `values/content.py:346` (2163, a field walk). The decision's "8 readers" does not reproduce; the text lists the two files and the encoder, assigns them to N03, and the 48 test reads (19 files, receiver text heuristic, an upper bound) to N03 as well (6.4). If the 8 counted a wider predicate, the builder's AST count at N03's opening decides.
- **SD-19 CONTRADICTION (amendment A1 against itself): `TensorProductSpace` is deleted at N04, not at N07.** Amendment A1 says N04 "REPLACES the concrete product (no second product type exists at any commit)" and that N07 deletes "`DualSpace`, `TensorProductSpace` and whatever N04 left". Both cannot hold. `[M]` `legacy_spy.json`: 489 `TensorProductSpace` constructions in 17 gate files, 489 axis-built and 0 non-axis, so no legacy class needs it; it is deleted at N04 and N07's list is `DualSpace`, the named-leaf arm and the allow-list (6.8).
- **SD-20 `DualSpace` lives until N07 although its live primals end at N06.** `[M]` `legacy_spy.json`: of 226 constructions 154 are duals of axis-built spaces (these become `TensorSpace` duals at N04), 66 duals of legacy leaves, 6 of a name-built space. The 66 need `DualSpace` until N06 deletes the leaves; deleting it at N06 would put the allow-list's last entry out in the unit that also deletes the six leaves, so N07 (the closing unit amendment A1 names) deletes it and the closed-sum gate lands there. Reason: one deletion per unit keeps each unit's red set separable.
- **SD-21 Constructors (amendment A5).** Read from `numerics/axis.py:461-640`: `Axis.declared` is replaced by the bare constructor with the `Declared` origin inside `TensorSpace.declared(name, shape, variance, *, form=None)`; `Axis.of(generator, label)` and `FactorGenerator` are replaced by the four existing constructors (`of_measure`, `of_quadrature`, `of_basis`, and the bare `Axis(..., origin=FromFrame(...))`) and contract 1 (section 4) is stated as the attributes the five `Origin` arms expose (`determined_weights` replaced by `form`); `Axis.restricted(indices)` is a new verb with a new identity field `restriction` (a frozen `Restriction(parent_key, indices)`, identity, not provenance), because amendment A2 asks that the parent be recorded in the identity while `origin` is excluded from it (`axis.py:461`); `Axis.of_quadrature(quadrature, label, *, face_axis=None)` reads the quadrature's own cosine along `face_axis` (`mean_axis_cosine`) and mints the face-weighted form `Diag(|cos| w)`, so that the origin stays `FromQuadrature` and no caller-supplied array can disagree with the quadrature (the #710 defect; orchestrator ruling, pass 1b). All are assigned to N03 (6.4).
- **SD-22 A flip on a sum with a legacy-leaf summand is refused (widened by pass 2).** N05 builds the direct sum while legacy leaves still exist, and a leaf holds no factor label, so `riesz(on={"angular"})` on the production full field (a bulk `TensorSpace` plus a legacy trace leaf) would flip the bulk alone and, at N06, both summands: one call with two meanings, which is L6's unspellable state. Every non-empty `on=` flip and every whole `riesz()` on a sum with a legacy-leaf summand is therefore refused ("a legacy leaf holds no factor labels"); the case disappears at N06 (check 2 elegance N05 C1, structure N05 F9).
- **SD-27 The variance of the production name-built mints (amendment A1), settled by the orchestrator (pass 1b).** `current(face)` is not a name-built mint: it is `summand.without_axis("angular")`, the face summand with its angular slot contracted, so its factors are the summand's own energy and spatial factors with their variances unchanged (3.6, 6.7, W1-P1). At N04, before `current(face)` exists, today's `angular_trace_space.py:685` `current_space` is rebuilt as the two-factor tensor space `<energy, spatial>` with the factors the face summand will carry (energy `(MEASURE, INTEGRATED)`, spatial `(MEASURE, DENSITY)`), so there is no placeholder variance at any commit (check 2 elegance N04 F8, structure N04 F7; `[R]` the shape consequence is an N04 opening measurement). `coordinate_space_of(shape)` (`numerics/space.py:1646`, the Euclidean coordinate space an unposed matrix acts on) is `Variance(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` with the counting form, a bare coordinate space with its Euclidean structure, no tag. The flattened space of `kernel_projection_gauge.py:171` declares `Variance(FUNCTION, DENSITY)` with the source's diagonal form and carries `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[nullspace-gauge] W2`: the gauge is the minimum-norm member of a coset, an operator kernel that wave W2 types (attack 2, cross-method note), and W1 leaves its coordinate space declared. For a mixed-kind source (a flux space is `MEASURE` on every slot, a response `FUNCTION`) it does not inherit a variance, it declares one.
- **SD-23 The `weld` debt entry of `FunctionSpace`** is edited in two steps: at N04 the declaration comment drops `factor` (`role: weld | welded: tensor, fmetric`) because the product arm and its axes leave; at N07 it becomes `role: component` of the concept `space` (the default of P2-13(a); `role: interface` would add a `role_test interface` row because the tool requires a `typing.Protocol` base, `[M]` `amend2/p2_13_simulate.out` variant a1) and the entry leaves the debt file, because the leaf arm (the metric storage) leaves then (8.2).
- **SD-24 The R14 flux row is minted at N04 on the production builders; the other three rows are derived at N08 and declared by production at N11.** Reason: amendment A1 forbids a placeholder; the flux row is what a layout declares for the trial space (D16), and the pair needs a trial only.
- **SD-25 `Declaration.contains` raises** `ValueError` with the fragment "subspace inclusion is undecided" where the sum cannot decide (amendment A5: no tri-state value). Reason: the two callers (`Subquotient` construction and `Factor`'s constructibility invariant, S6 and P2-1, which replaced the legality test) both refuse on undecided, so a boolean plus a refusal loses nothing.
- **SD-26 Gate ids follow the new unit numbers.** `G<unit><letter>` is renumbered with the units; the old-to-new gate table is in the amendment record. Reason: a gate id that names a unit that is not its unit misleads a builder (the old id `G06j` would now sit in N07).

**Choices carried from the first draft (renumbered `SD-`)**

- **SD-1 `Variance` is a class.** The pair `(kind, chart)` with its two involutions is the Klein four-set; grouping it gives the concept `dual` a head that obeys its kind (`set`) and puts the group law in one class, instead of two loose enum fields on the factor. D1 names the fields, not their grouping; the fields are unchanged.
- **SD-2 `Declaration` arms are `Coord`, `Spanned`, `Ann`.** D11 names the sum `Coord(I)`, `Declared(generator key)`, `Ann(.)`; `Declared` is already an `Origin` arm at `numerics/axis.py:265` (two classes of one name in one package), so the generator-declared arm is renamed `Spanned` and carries `(n, key, dim)`. The numeric basis is never stored in a declaration (a basis would be a float array in an identity key).
- **SD-3 The scalar and radial traces are not pushforwards of the angular trace (amendment A8).** D23 is `[R]` and its first test ran: `[M]` `measure_trace_metric.md` (d), `m_h = (M/4)(1+err)`, with ratios 1.0425, 1.0115, 1.1547; so the scalar weight `A_f` is the pushforward only if `M = 4` and the rule is exact. Refuted FOR "is today's scalar trace weight the pushforward of `|mu| w`"; the fact it establishes is that the normalisation is a choice. The hemispheric trace is built by the same builder shape with its own two-point angular axis whose stored coordinates are partial currents; W1 states no pushforward law and chooses no normalisation; the former user item U10 is a recorded input of W7 (section 9) with its numbers. The radial seed is a state gauge (`V(R)`), kept with its values.
- **SD-4 The 15 numpy norm sites are not re-pointed (C3).** The panel's claim "re-pointing the norms moves no number" is refuted at 10 of 15 sites (differ on 13 to 31 percent of hits, up to 3.5e-15) and the typed norm moves the convergence-test norms on 0 of 47,613 hits to bit equality; they are coordinate norms; the convergence norms are W5's. Two completions are named (`euclidean()` for energy, `counting()` for all forms); the 2 production `space.norm` sites use `euclidean()` and are bit-equal. This refines D6 and does not contradict it.
- **SD-5 Units are serial, N01 with N02 the only parallel pair.** D30 rules that a serial chain may re-edit a merged file; so the three space-hub units (N04, N05, N06) are separate serial units, each green on its own: `[M]` `incidence_amend1.json`: the pairs share 19 (N04 and N05), 14 (N04 and N06) and 13 (N05 and N06) production files; the first draft's order (direct sum, trace, space-core) put 3 types on a placeholder and edited `trace.py` twice (check findings E1, S7, S8); the leaves-first order of amendment A1 removes both.
- **SD-8 The form of a `FromBasis` axis.** The continuum Gram of a basis where the family has one is a diagonal (`basis.coefficient_weights`, m2 4.1); where the family has none the axis's form is `NoForm`. Which families have one is the builder's read of `numerics/basis/` at N03's opening and is `[R]` here (not measured for this draft). A consumer that relies on a formed basis axis finds out at N03's G03g (bytes of the form weights).
- **SD-9 The store is regenerated once, at W1's close.** `[M]` `measure_callsites.md` 4.2: each edit of a pinned file stales the same 2117 entries and the regenerated entries are staled by the next such edit, so a regeneration per merge is wasted; the witness comparison runs once after N11.
- **SD-10 `FaceLayout` is renamed `CoproductLayout`, `FaceSlot` is renamed `LayoutBlock`.** D13 says the offsets are computed once by a coproduct arm of `Layout` and that `FaceLayout` is absorbed; the existing class is already the ordered coproduct of named blocks (generic in its key). Renaming keeps one object and removes a face-specific name from a general one; the alternative (keep the name) leaves a trace word inside every direct sum. The 54 test sites and 7 production files are in N05's set.
- **SD-11 The radial characteristic spaces are re-spelled in W1, as builder functions, and listed under `rayspace` (amendment A7).** `design_algebra.md` left them to W4; but `FunctionSpace` is closed on two arms (D14) and they subclass the weld, so the classes cannot remain. They are not retired as a concept: N06 re-spells them as two functions in `numerics/spaces/radial_characteristic_space.py`, `radial_interior_space(levels, *, ng, nx, cell_volumes)` and `radial_boundary_space(levels, *, ng, nx, cell_volumes)` (3.6), each returning an axis-built `DirectSumSpace` with unchanged values, and the concept `rayspace` (W4's) lists them, not `trace`.
- **SD-12 N09 re-points four pairings and N10 the fifth.** HP5 (`sn/solver.py:1756`) is both a hand pairing and the leakage that must count `A_f` once and carry the orientation; one line, one unit: N10 owns it. HP3, HP4, HP6, HP10 are bit-equal (HP6 within 1.8e-16) and are N09's.
- **SD-13 Where `apply_metric` and `apply_inverse_metric` go.** They stay as coordinate-map names (99 and 30 test sites, 7 and 2 production sites) and are defined as `flat_on(formed labels)` and `sharp_on(formed labels)`: today's behaviour, equal to the typed Riesz legs on the formed blocks (amendment A5). Whole-space `flat` and `sharp` refuse a `NoForm` block, as D6 says; `apply_metric` is not a whole-space `flat`.
- **SD-14 New classes in the content-identity roster.** `Factor`, `Subquotient`, `Restriction` and the declaration arms are `ContentIdentity` subclasses (as `Axis` is, `orpheus/values/content.py:482`) and are rostered with the unit that creates them (the declaration arms at N01, the others at N03; P2-6); else `test_s5_9_the_population_is_the_roster` cannot see them. `[R]`: the mixin's exact encoder contract was not read for this draft.
- **SD-15 Gate files live in `tests/gates/numerics/w1/`.** The layout of `tests/gates/` mirrors `orpheus/`; the gates concern `numerics/` and one `transport/` file; two directories keep W1's gates findable: the `transport/` gates (`FourSpaces`, the coarse-balance gate, the production pair) live in `tests/gates/transport/w1/`, because `tests/gates/test_test_layers.py` holds files under `tests/gates/numerics/` to `numerics`' import rights (check 2 structure N08 F1b, `[R]` read, not run). The existing re-pointed gates stay where they are.
- **SD-43 The form of `EnergyAxis` is stated by the arm, never defaulted from its origin (ruled, pass 3).** 3.2 gives `EnergyAxis` the origin `Declared(NODAL)`, and a `Declared` axis given no form takes counting (D5); read together they would give energy the counting form, against D6 and U2. Ruled: `EnergyAxis` sets `form = NoForm` itself, and the counting default applies only to a constructor that is given no form on an axis that is not the energy arm. N03's opening states the line of `numerics/axis.py` that implements the default and shows `EnergyAxis` does not pass through it (gate G03c).
- **SD-16 Declarations of the new classes.** Each new class carries the literal comment of section 3; two role choices are not in any ruling of the synthesis: `NoForm` is a `component` of `fmetric`, and `Axis` stays `role: weld` of `factor` while `EnergyAxis` keeps its present role (P2b-3, SD-41).

## 12. What is left out, with the structural reason, and the open measurements

**Left out.**

| left out | structural reason | where it goes |
|---|---|---|
| operators on true ends: the Riesz legs as operators, the adjoint from the pairing, `CoupledOperator`, injections and restrictions as operators, retraction/section/pullback, the 12 hand retractions | operators are linear maps between registry spaces (O38); W1 supplies the spaces and leg data | W2 |
| member classes, `CompositeField`, `CoupledField`, `RolePair`, `FieldRole`, the cell-moment factor, a typed role on a member | members follow their space; W1 holds one carrier hook | W3 |
| the merge rule in production (the `psid` carrier of `mixture.py:582-586`: preserves `<psid, psi>` instead of `<psid, q>`, 7.1e-3 non-uniform, 1.6e-3 uniform; the adjoint-arm balance, 7.5e-3 and 2.1e-3, a documented trade T4) | W8b owns the rule; W1 owns the slot data and the toy gate | W8b |
| the fibre form (a form block with more than one slot: the curvilinear in-cell Gram) | no consumer today (curvilinear SN is deferred by ruling); building it first is a premature abstraction | `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[fibre-form]`, W3 |
| the chart of in-cell and face moments (the cell-moment factor) | the face-moment factor of a trace summand carries `(MEASURE, DENSITY)` with the counting form (P2-3); which chart the in-cell and face moments are stored in is the cell-moment factor's question | `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[cell-moment]`, W3 |
| a general idempotent subspace (the Reynolds projector of an orbit space) and a Moore-Penrose gauge as a default | needs a declared complement; the quotient by a symmetry group is W6's; the gauge is a named object, never a default (D10) | W6, W2 |
| a space-level subquotient (only `kernel_projection_gauge`, an operator kernel with `K` not inside `ker G`, needs one) | A2 B4: a tensor of slot subquotients has a non-rectangular kernel | the fallback named in A2 second pass: a second arm beside the factor field, if the user wants it in W1 |
| the Krein (indefinite) form | no producer: 0 of 16 shipped rules carry a negative weight `[M]` | named, refused |
| `DiscreteMeasure.__add__` over differing supports, a typed `Face`, the quadrature's reflection as a verb | measures and generators; faces | W8a, W6 |
| the storage change of the trace to the quotient (128 to 64) and re-pointing the boundary members and operators | moves flat dimension and every trace operator | W7 and Step B |
| a pushforward law between the angular and the scalar trace, and a normalisation of the hemispheric trace (amendment A8) | `[M]` the scalar weight is not the pushforward of `abs(mu) w` (ratios 1.0425, 1.0115, 1.1547); the normalisation is a choice | W7 (section 9, recorded input) |
| the two bare sums of the eigenvalue estimator, HP1 and HP2, and the convergence norms | the estimator's functional and the iteration's norms are W5's (amendment C1, C3) | W5 |
| renaming `FunctionSpace` to `Space` | a name, not a structural fact: 53 production and 107 test files | not scheduled |
| a shim keeping any retired class name | a shim is a twin (chart ruling A2); the named-leaf arm kept under `ELEGANCE-DEBT[legacy-leaf] N07` is the old code not yet deleted, not an adapter (SD-18) | forbidden |
| `derivations/` and `tools/` re-pointing | `[M]` 0 of 32 of 33 rows read there | none needed |

**Refuted candidates** are in the synthesis (section 2 there) with their structural reasons and are not repeated; this draft adds: the pushforward identification of the scalar trace (SD-3, refuted FOR "is today's scalar weight the pushforward"; the fact: the normalisation is a choice); the claim that the numpy norms re-point to `euclidean()` without moving a number (SD-4, refuted at 10 of 15 sites); the 448-site reading of hand-built dense metrics (`[M]` AST: 0 production bare calls and 63 test sites in 18 files; 448 is a runtime construction count); the orientation-blind retraction as the leakage (`[M]` `sign_report.out`: it computes `J+ + J-`, off by `(1 + alpha)/(1 - alpha)` from today's net current: refuted FOR "is the leakage the retraction by `Diag(abs(cos) * w)`"; the fact: the sign `sigma_f` is a member of the function space on the angular index set and enters through the partner function); the first draft's two-step typing of the trace (refuted FOR "does the trace need two mints": with the tensor core built first it is minted once, SD-5); a shim class for the five legacy leaves (SD-18).

**Open measurements (each `[R]` above until run).**
1. The `ng >= 2` probe of the trace group axis (`measure_pairings.md` section 4): closed for `ng = 2` `[M]` (`check_structure_N06.md` 10, `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/check2/n06_probe2.py`: 8 of 8 cases have trace weights byte-equal to `kron(|Omega.n| w, ones)` in slot order angular, energy, spatial, tail, and the energy-first control reads different on 8 of 8); `ng = 3` remains G06c's arm, not run.
2. Whether the Rayleigh quotient `k` moves under a typed HP1: not needed by W1 (HP1 and HP2 are tagged for W5); W5's.
3. The pinned numbers that read `.matrix` directly (tests and files outside the 101-file sample; `measure_inverse_bytes.md` (c)): N01's opening.
4. The 158-file population of trace-metric readers (N10 opening): the 12 reds are a lower bound.
5. Which `FromBasis` families have a continuum Gram (SD-8).
6. The runtime enumeration of the consumers of the five metric verbs on an energy-bearing space (N11 opening); the AST count is an upper bound.
7. Whether `PositionedMetric` has a reader after the overlay goes (N04 opening).
8. The live-entry growth of the memo store since W0 (101 entries, unexplained): measured at N00, before anything is edited (C4).
9. The count of `Axis(` sites passing `weights=` (N03 opening; `Axis(` bare is 176 test sites in 40 files, an upper bound).
10. The three fixture builders of the harness (N00): named from the files the spy population used, not re-read for this draft.
11. The post-W1 reference-cycle graph (8.3 "after" is `[R]`; re-measured at the end of N07 and N11).
12. The after-the-fact check that no Step A marker XPASSes (W1-P6) is a prediction (`[R]`) until the first unit runs it.
13. The HP6 spy over `tests/gates/derivations/` and `tests/gates/reference/` (N09 opening): the 15-file population of 6.10 found no pinned number that reads HP6 (`[M]` `hp6_report.out`), and those two directories were not run.
14. The split of the 8 `DualSpace`-naming test files (41 sites) by the receiver of the dual (N04 opening): axis-built receivers are N04's, legacy-leaf receivers N07's.
15. The variance each of the 265 name-built test sites declares (N04 codemod): sites that never meet `dual`, `riesz` or a pairing take `(FUNCTION, DENSITY)`; the 24 files that call `.dual(` (143 sites) are migrated by hand.
16. The census of formed factors whose form has a zero weight and a whole `sk` (P2-1): dead-mode frame axes, zero-weight test fixtures, quadratures (N03 opening, N04 opening); each is a mint that must state `V/kernel`.
17. The same census at the production builders of 3.4a (a zero cell volume, a zero Gram entry: `check_structure_N03.md`, unexplored: `base.py:489` "zero on padding").
18. An `Axis` of index shape `()` through `layout`, `_axis_index`, `AxisRetractionOperator` and `Factor` (P2-2; N06's opening, SD-31).
19. The runtime readers of the stored field `name` of a space (P2-14; N07's opening; the AST bound is 28 reads in 13 production files).
20. The cutoff at `moc/geometry.py:143` (it stays, P2b-5; the orchestrator records it on issue #750), N02's opening read of the two added cutoffs (SD-30) and the opening runs of the exact-zero census of `mu_x` on the curvilinear rules before `_MU_DIRECTION_EPS` is deleted `[R]`.

## 13. Amendment record

Pass 1 of the specification, 2026-11-23, applied from `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amendment_decisions_1.md` on the first draft (`b5f18d4e`, kept at `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend1/spec_before.md`). Those ids are the decisions file's (decisions A1 to A8, B1 to B4, C1 to C5 and D); the check findings E# are `check_elegance.md`'s and the findings S# of "the structure check" are `check_structure.md`'s (neither is a section object of 2.1, which were `S1` to `S12` at pass 1 and are `S1` to `S13` now). Measurements taken for the pass are under `scratch/ontology/w1/spec/amend1/`; each is cited where it is used and listed here by decision.

**One line per decision: the sections it changed.**

- **A1** (E1, S7, S8): header; 1 (Outcome, W1-P6, W1-P8, W1-P9); 3.1 (Variance promotion, Factor row), 3.4 (FunctionSpace and TensorSpace rows, the energy window), 3.5; 4; 5 (unit column, FunctionSpace and TensorProductSpace rows); 6 and 7 rewritten on the new unit order; 8 (unit column); 10; 11 (SD-5, SD-18 to SD-20, SD-23, SD-24); 12. Measured: the legacy classes' place under the abstract type (`legacy_inherit2.out`, `legacy_arms.out`), the `TensorProductSpace` and `DualSpace` construction census (`legacy_spy.json`), the name-built constructor census (`namebuilt_sites.out`, `namebuilt_count2.out`), the incidence on the new boundaries (`incidence_amend1.out`). The `FromFrame` row moved to N03.
- **A2** (E2, S3, S4, E13): 2.1 (S3, S5, S6), 2.2 (L3, L9, L10), 2.4; 3.1 (`Subquotient.dim`, `Factor.stored_shape`), 3.2 (`Axis.restricted`, the `restriction` identity field), 3.6 (which half-tier spaces become restricted axes: read from `angular_trace_space.py:598-706`), 3.7 (the subquotient clause); 6.7; 7.4 (G03b, G03k), 7.5 (G04j), 7.10 (G09a); 11 (the old SD-7 is deleted).
- **A3** (S1, E3, E10, S10): 2.2 (L15), 2.4; 3.6 (`Orientation.sign()`, `K` read from the form's kernel, the face identity is its label); 6.3, 6.11; 7.3 (G02b), 7.7 (G06d, G06g, G06h), 7.11 (G10b). Measured: the sphere-albedo sign probe (`sign_report.out`) and the zero-weight census (`zero_weight_census.out`).
- **A4** (E4, S2): 2.1 (S6 legality once, S7 the chart of the form), 2.3 (the normal-form rule), 3.1 (`Factor.form`, the `sk` of a frame factor); 7.4 (G03a, G03f); 10 (U1 rewording).
- **A5** (E5 to E9, E11, E12, E14, E16, E18): 2.3 (`Form` one definition, `contains` raises), 3.2 (constructors, `Axis.weights` retired), 3.3, 3.4 (`declared`, `euclidean`, `counting`, `axis` and `axes`, `flat_on`, `sharp_on`, the delegation), 3.5 (`partition_by` built in N05), 3.7 (`covector` returns a `Covector`), 4 (contract 1 without `FactorGenerator`), 5; 6.4, 6.5, 6.12; 7.4, 7.5 (G04l); 9; 11 (SD-17, SD-21, SD-25). Measured: the readers of `Axis.weights` (`weights_readers.out`, `axisw_spy.json`).
